Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"柱面测地线流匹配"方法，用于准周期心血管波形（如PPG、SCG）之间的配对翻译，通过在相位-振幅柱面上定义闭式测地线替代标准仿射路径，消除振幅与瞬时频率失真，并优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备在不同身体位置采集的心血管信号之间存在源到目标的翻译需求。这类准周期信号具有内在几何结构：相位为循环变量、振幅严格为正、逐个心搏的对齐会不可预测地漂移。现有深度神经网络方法未显式学习配对信号间的相位传输，监督回归和流匹配中标准的仿射路径均未考虑这种相位-振幅结构。

Method: 提出柱面测地线流匹配（cylindrical geodesic flow matching）。指出标准仿射插值路径在准周期信号间插值时会扭曲中间振幅和瞬时频率，因此改用相位-振幅柱面上的闭式测地线路径，从而消除上述伪影，并将每对训练样本转化为稠密且几何一致的"速度"监督信号。

Result: 在零样本PPG和有限支撑SCG适配基准上，方法一致优于插值基线，并达到或超过直接监督预测；相比最强竞争基线，Hilbert变换、L2和动态时间规整距离最多降低约15%。

Conclusion: 桥接路径的几何结构是振荡信号翻译中流匹配的关键归纳偏置，柱面测地线建模为相位-振幅耦合信号提供了一种有效且几何一致的方法。

Abstract: Paired translation between quasiperiodic physiological waveforms (i.e., recovering a target oscillatory signal from the source) is central to the interpretation of cardiovascular signals derived from wearables placed at different body locations. This source-to-target mapping in these problems carries inherent geometric structure: the phase wraps around the cycle and must be treated as a circular variable, the amplitude remains strictly positive, and the beat-to-beat alignment can drift unpredictably across cycles and subjects. While deep neural networks have been used for phase estimation and complex-valued signal modeling, prior work does not explicitly learn phase transport between paired signals. Consequently, neither endpoint-supervised regression nor the standard affine path used in flow matching accounts for this phase--amplitude structure. We introduce \emph{cylindrical geodesic flow matching} for paired cardiovascular waveform translation. We show that the standard affine path used in flow matching distorts intermediate amplitude and instantaneous frequency when interpolating between quasiperiodic signals; replacing it with a closed-form geodesic on the phase--amplitude cylinder eliminates these artifacts and converts each training pair into dense, geometry-consistent velocity supervision. On zero-shot photoplethysmography and limited-support seismocardiography adaptation benchmarks, our method consistently outperforms interpolation baselines and matches or exceeds direct supervised prediction, reducing Hilbert Transform, $L_2$, and Dynamic Time Warping distance by up to ${\sim}15\%$ over the strongest competing baseline. These results suggest that bridge geometry is a critical inductive bias for flow matching on oscillatory signal translation.

</details>


### [355] [AnyBottle: A Recipe to Only Keep the Concepts You Really Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08552)
*Wolfgang Stammer,Sukrut Rao,Hevra Petekkaya,David Steinmann,Bernt Schiele*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnyBottle提出一种构建紧凑、任务特定概念瓶颈模型（CBM）的统一方法，无需概念标注，通过黑盒教师引导从无监督概念池中选择概念，实现少概念、高一致性的可解释预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始CBM需要人工概念标注；无标注变体虽免除标注，但通常使用庞大且固定（训练和推理时均固定）的概念词表，导致瓶颈远大于任务或预测所需，且难以检查。因此需要一个能构建紧凑、任务特定瓶颈的方案。

Method: AnyBottle仅假设有冻结骨干网络和无监督概念池（如稀疏自编码器）。用同一骨干上训练的黑盒教师指导选择：每轮加入最能解释当前瓶颈失败的概念，候选限制在教师与学生分歧区域。使用嵌套dropout按选择顺序训练，使最终瓶颈在任意概念前缀下都能准确预测，从而在推理时可少用概念（对早自信的输入）或多用概念（对困难输入）。由于各阶段不依赖特定模态，新领域/任务只需替换骨干和概念池。

Result: 在六个视觉数据集和两个文本数据集、两种教师范式上，AnyBottle相比无标注基线产生更少概念且概念一致性更高的瓶颈，同时性能接近黑盒参考。

Conclusion: AnyBottle表明无标注不必意味着大瓶颈：一个小型、被发掘出的词表可以像大得多的固定词表一样具有表达力。

Abstract: Concept bottleneck models (CBMs) make predictions inspectable and intervenable by routing them through human-interpretable concepts, but originally required concept annotations. Annotation-free variants remove this requirement, but typically use large concept vocabularies, static at both training and inference, producing bottlenecks larger than any task or prediction needs and harder to inspect. We propose AnyBottle, a single recipe for building compact, task-specific CBMs. AnyBottle assumes only a frozen backbone and an unsupervised concept pool, such as a sparse autoencoder. A black-box teacher trained on the same backbone then guides selection: each round adds the concept that best explains the bottleneck's current failures, with candidates restricted to regions of teacher/student disagreement. Trained with nested dropout over this selection order, the final bottleneck predicts accurately from any concept prefix, so inference spends fewer concepts on inputs it is confident about early and more on hard ones. Since no stage is modality-specific, a new domain and task requires swapping only the backbone and concept pool. Across six vision and two text datasets and two teacher paradigms, AnyBottle yields bottlenecks with fewer concepts and higher concept consistency than annotation-free baselines, while staying close to the black-box reference. Overall, AnyBottle shows that going annotation-free need not mean going large: a small, discovered vocabulary can be as expressive as a much larger, fixed one.

</details>


### [356] [Adaptive Power Sampling for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08563)
*Bingnan Xiao,Chenhao Yang,Bingcong Li,Wei Ni,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自适应功率采样(APS),按查询难度动态调整锐化指数,无需训练即可提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列级功率采样方法对整个查询集合统一锐化基座模型分布,忽略了查询难度差异以及基座模型对每个查询的已有处理能力。

Method: 理论分析表明进一步锐化的收益由正确与错误响应之间的自奖励差距决定;据此提出APS,在测试时基于答案一致性与模型自奖励的关系,对每个查询单独调整锐化指数。

Result: 在MATH500、HumanEval、GPQA等多种推理任务上,APS持续优于固定锐化指数的功率采样,且无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 为功率采样引入查询自适应能力可在零训练成本下稳定提升LLM推理表现。

Abstract: Sequence-level power sampling has recently emerged as a training-free approach to reasoning by sampling from a sharpened output distribution of a base large language model (LLM). Nevertheless, existing methods typically sharpen the base model distribution uniformly across queries, overlooking variations in query difficulty and in how well the base model already handles each query. The goal of this work is to equip power sampling with query adaptivity. Theoretically, we show that the benefits of further sharpening are determined by the self-reward gap between correct and incorrect responses. Based on this insight, we propose \emph{Adaptive Power Sampling} (APS), which adjusts the sharpening exponent on a per-query basis at test time using the relationship between answer agreement and the model's self-reward. Experiments across diverse reasoning tasks, including MATH500, HumanEval, and GPQA, show that APS consistently outperforms power sampling with a fixed sharpening exponent, without additional training.

</details>


### [357] [MINDSET: Energy-based Schema Evolution for Long Conversational Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08586)
*Sujato Dutta,Sreekruthy Tummala,Shashank Vanga,Ayushmi Pavani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MINDSET将长期对话记忆建模为受约束的状态管理：把对话存为不可变episode并按最小能量状态转移组织成版本化schema，在LoCoMo与MemoryAgentBench上取得最高F1及检索排名提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长对话智能体需记住很久以前的内容以避免用户重复指令和上下文，但指令与上下文会随时间变化，智能体必须随之适应。理想记忆系统需同时保留当前与历史状态、区分过时信息与有效知识、按查询检索合适证据，并避免反复调用大模型重写交互历史。

Method: 提出MINDSET记忆控制器：将对话存储为不可变episode，并通过最小能量状态转移组织为带版本的schema。每个新episode可强化、取代、拆分或新建schema；转移决策权衡表示失真、矛盾、历史损害、碎片化和内部不一致，并用迟滞机制防止孤立矛盾过早改写稳定记忆。

Result: 在850题可复现样本（700 LoCoMo + 150 MemoryAgentBench）上与5种记忆系统对比：LoCoMo答案F1最高，检索排序（Recall@8、MRR、nDCG@8）显著优于次优方法LightMem（Holm校正后p<0.01）；MemoryAgentBench得分最高但相对差距较小。消融显示受控碎片化与schema感知分配对答案质量贡献最大；700题跨模型评估（GLM-4.7与Gemma-4-31B）支持模型无关性。

Conclusion: 长期记忆更适合作为受约束的状态管理来处理，而非持续摘要。MINDSET以版本化schema与最小能量转移兼顾当前/历史状态并区分过时信息，验证了该范式在答案质量、检索排序与模型独立性上的有效性。

Abstract: Long conversational agents have become essential in our daily lives. They must remember what was said long back in order to help us efficiently complete a task without needing the user to repeat instructions and context repeatedly. However, the main issue is that instructions and context change over time and so the agents must be able to adapt accordingly. A useful memory system should preserve both current and historical states, distinguish stale information from active knowledge, retrieve evidence appropriate to the query and avoid repeatedly invoking a large language model to rewrite prior interactions. We introduce MINDSET, a memory controller that stores a conversation as immutable episodes and organizes them into versioned schemas through minimum-energy state transitions. Each incoming episode may reinforce, supersede, split or create a schema. The transition decision balances representation distortion, contradiction, historical damage, fragmentation and internal inconsistency, while hysteresis prevents isolated contradictions from prematurely rewriting stable memory. We evaluate MINDSET against 5 memory systems on a reproducible sample of 850 questions (700 LoCoMo + 150 MemoryAgentBench). MINDSET obtains the highest observed LoCoMo answer F1 while significantly improving retrieval ranking (Recall@8, MRR and nDCG@8) over the second best method LightMem (p<0.01 after Holm correction). It obtains the highest observed scores on MemoryAgentBench although the relative difference is low. Ablations identify controlled fragmentation and schema-aware assignment as the largest contributors to answer quality. Additionally, a 700-question cross-model evaluation with GLM-4.7 and Gemma-4-31B supported model independence. These results show that long-term memory can be better handled as constrained state management rather than continual summarization.

</details>


### [358] [Parallel Predictive World Models for Accurate and Efficient Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08627)
*Wanjin Feng,Baobin Zhang,Ao Yu,Shibo Feng,Xi Wang,Xingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并行预测世界模型（PPWM），并行预测有限时域轨迹并保留未来表示间的因果交互，无需逐步自回归，实现更低长时域预测误差和更快CEM规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统长时域世界模型规划依赖自回归展开，逐步反馈预测状态会形成随长度增长的串行路径，并将后续预测暴露于递归解码状态反馈，导致误差累积和效率低下。

Method: PPWM并行预测有限时域轨迹：每个时域以其因果动作前缀为条件，未来表示在解码前先进行因果交互，将时间因果性与逐状态输出递归解耦；并形式化地将自回归展开视为因果轨迹映射，辨识出PPWM移除的解码状态反馈路径。

Result: 在四个视觉控制任务中，PPWM在所评估的预测接口中取得最低的长时域预测误差和最高的CEM模拟器成功率，同时相比自回归LeWM基线平均实现超过3倍的CEM规划加速。

Conclusion: 准确高效的长时域世界模型规划不必然需要逐状态自回归，可通过并行的因果轨迹预测实现。

Abstract: Long-horizon world-model planning typically relies on autoregressive rollouts, where predicted states are repeatedly fed back into the model. This preserves temporal structure but creates a horizon-length sequential path and exposes later predictions to recursive decoded-state feedback. We introduce Parallel Predictive World Models (PPWM), which predict a finite-horizon trajectory in parallel while retaining causal interaction among future representations. Each horizon is conditioned on its causal action prefix, and future representations interact before decoding, separating temporal causality from state-by-state output recursion. We formalize this distinction by viewing autoregressive rollout as a causal trajectory map and identifying the decoded-state feedback pathway removed by PPWM. Across four visual-control tasks, PPWM achieves the lowest long-horizon prediction error and the highest Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) simulator success among the evaluated predictive interfaces. Meanwhile, PPWM achieves more than a 3$\times$ average CEM planning speedup over the autoregressive LeWM baseline. These results suggest that accurate and efficient long-horizon world-model planning does not require state-by-state autoregression, but can instead be achieved through parallel causal trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [359] [Coupled but Late: Turn-Taking Between Full-Duplex Speech Models in Unscripted Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08683)
*Lichen Zhu,Yueqian Lin,Yiheng Wang,Hai "Helen" Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 两个PersonaPlex-7B全双工语音模型互相对话，其轮流时机耦合但底轮流延迟显著，表现为"感知结束后反应"而非人类的"话轮结束预测"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音模型越来越多地被用于模型间对话（自博弈、智能体社会、模型评估），此时没有人吸收时机误差——每个模型的轮流就是另一个的输入。作者想探究这种闭环最终稳定在何种时机模式，并与人类对话进行对比。

Method: 让两个PersonaPlex-7B实例在共享时钟上交换音频token进行无脚本对话，并应用统一的底轮流（floor-transfer）规则对模型对话与Switchboard人类对话进行比较分析。还进行了解配对（re-pairing）实验和单向通道延迟实验。

Result: 模型轮流时机是耦合的：跨对话重新配对说话人会破坏这种耦合。但底轮流发生较晚，中位数为400-560 ms，而人类为137 ms；在伙伴话轮最后120 ms内（人类预测性转移中占十分之一的位置），模型仅有1%的转移。单向延迟通道会使响应一对一移动，且其前的过渡区变空。

Conclusion: 模型的轮流机制与人类不同：它表现为对感知到的话轮结束作出反应性等待，而非人类时机所需的"话轮结束预测"机制。

Abstract: Full-duplex speech models are trained to converse with a person, but they are increasingly made to converse with each other, in self-play data generation, agent societies, and model-based evaluation. In that loop no human absorbs a timing error: each model's turn-taking is the other's input. We ask what timing the loop settles into. Two PersonaPlex-7B instances exchange audio tokens on a shared clock in unscripted conversation, and one floor-transfer rule is applied to them and to Switchboard. Their timing is coupled: re-pairing speakers across conversations destroys it. But the floor changes hands late, at a median of 400-560 ms against 137 ms for humans, and the last 120 ms of the partner's turn, where human projection places a tenth of its transfers, holds 1% of theirs. Delaying one direction of the channel shifts the response one-for-one and leaves the run-up to it empty, consistent with a reactive wait after the perceived end rather than the turn-end projection human timing requires.

</details>


### [360] [ScienceClaw: Benchmarking Continual Self-Evolution of AI-for-Science Agents Across the Natural and Social Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08691)
*Mingda Zhang,Wenjin Liu,Tiesunlong Shen,Zikai Xiao,Zhenghong Lin,Qing Xu,Erik Cambria,Xiaoying Tang,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScienceClaw将科学任务求解、验证与程序更新统一为固定参数的程序自进化框架，并配套跨23学科的ScienceClaw-Eval评测体系，衡量科学正确性、进化增益、保持、跨数据集迁移和进化成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体虽加速科学自动化，但经验证的执行很少转化为持久、程序级的改进，且现有评测未在自然科学与社会科学中考察连续任务下的这一过程。

Method: 形式化ScienceClaw为固定参数程序自进化，统一任务求解、科学验证与程序更新；通过多轮交互修复可执行工作流，将经再执行验证的失败—成功轨迹转化为关联的Skill与Operator候选，仅当源任务重放复现修复且独立科学任务改进时才保留更新。

Result: ScienceClaw-Eval覆盖23个学科，通过顺序任务流与独立重置评估，测量科学正确性、进化增益、保持、跨数据集迁移与进化成本。

Conclusion: 该工作提出将验证过的执行转化为持久程序级改进的框架与评测基准，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language model agents are accelerating scientific automation, yet verified executions rarely become persistent program-level improvements, and existing evaluations do not examine this process across sequential tasks in both the natural and social sciences. We formalize ScienceClaw as fixed-parameter program self-evolution that unifies task solving, scientific verification, and program updates. ScienceClaw-Eval spans 23 disciplines and measures scientific correctness, evolutionary gain, retention, cross-dataset transfer, and evolution cost through sequential streams and independent reset evaluation. Our framework repairs executable workflows through multi-turn interaction, converts re-execution-verified failure--success trajectories into linked Skill and Operator candidates, and retains an update only when source-task replay reproduces the repair and independent scientific tasks improve. Code is available at https://github.com/beita6969/ScienceClaw.

</details>


### [361] [nanoMuse: An Open-Source Personal Agent for Every Device You Own](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08699)
*Guangyi Liu,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文界定"个人代理"（personal agent）概念，剖析Meta的Muse闭源实现，并提出开源对应物nanoMuse及路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Meta的Muse是2026年面向单人的闭源云代理，具备账号、设备、记忆与持续对话能力，却无开源对应物。作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 用五个问题和三个时间视野定义个人代理；依据Meta公开记录及一份生产提示词副本，逐条标注来源地分析Muse的构建方式。

Result: 提出nanoMuse：GPL-3.0开源，一个代理覆盖用户所有设备，可操作手机与电脑屏幕；经任何人可自建的relay共享同一对话，所有动作经Sentinel，记忆为可读文件，模型可自选；给出规模与成本的估算。

Conclusion: 以"开放性、带出处的记忆、面向操作能力的评测套件及相应开源模型"作为路线图，为个人代理提供开放替代方案。

Abstract: Assistants from 2011 answered and waited, and agents from 2023 did a task and stopped. In September 2026 Meta's Muse showed an agent for one person, with accounts, devices, memory and a conversation that lasts, closed, in a vendor's cloud, in one country. Such an agent is expected to act on a person's accounts and devices, remember them across weeks, speak first when it is worth it, and answer for what it did. It is a kind of software, not a model, and until now had no open counterpart. This report defines the personal agent in five questions and three horizons. It reads how Muse is built from Meta's public record and a copy of its production prompt, each statement marked by its source. It then presents nanoMuse, the open-source counterpart under the GPL-3.0, one agent on every device a person owns, with hands on the phone's screen and the computer's. They share one conversation over a relay anyone can run; every action goes through a Sentinel, memory is files the person can read, and the model is their choice. Its size and cost are given as estimates. What is open, memory with provenance, an evaluation suite for the hands and an open model for them, is set out as a roadmap.

</details>


### [362] [WorldSolver: Can LLM Agents Simulate the Physical Dynamics via Solver Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08720)
*Siru Jiang,Yongzhe Lyu,Shuo Lu,Yubin Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Yue Liao,Bin Wang,Jian Liang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldSolver是首个从61篇经典图形学论文中构建的168个物理仿真求解器生成任务基准，覆盖7大物理域。评估显示前沿LLM智能体最好的成绩仅48.7%，凸显求解器生成任务的高难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建物理仿真求解器需要物理理解、数学推理与软件工程三方面能力，是检验LLM智能体综合能力的实用场景，但此前缺乏系统性基准。

Method: 从61篇经典计算机图形学论文中提取168个仿真任务，覆盖7个物理域；每个任务提供固定仿真环境的代码脚手架，智能体需补全求解器实现。从三个维度评估：执行检查、视觉保真度、物理合理性。

Result: 前沿智能体生成可执行求解器本身就很困难，满足视觉与物理正确性更难。GPT-5.6-Sol与Claude-Opus-5表现最佳，但总分仅48.7%和46.7%。

Conclusion: WorldSolver是迈向智能体自动生成求解器的早期一步，有望推动能够忠实模拟动态物理世界的智能体研究。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly advancing scientific and engineering problem solving, with physics simulation emerging as a challenging yet practical testbed for reproducing complex physical phenomena with application in embodied AI, games and films. As the workhorse of such simulation, a solver computes how the state of a dynamic system evolves over time. Building such solvers requires physical understanding to identify appropriate models, mathematical reasoning to formulate the underlying dynamics, and software engineering to implement them as executable code, yet this capability of LLM agents remains underexplored. To this end, we introduce WorldSolver, a benchmark of 168 simulation tasks derived from physical phenomena in 61 classic computer graphics papers, spanning 7 physical domains. Each task contains a code scaffold that provides a fixed simulation environment for the scene, with the solver implementation left for the agent to complete. Specifically, we evaluate them along three dimensions: Execution Checks for successful execution, Visual Fidelity for reproducing the intended dynamic behavior in the rendered simulation, and Physical Plausibility for physics-grounded verification of the generated dynamics. Experiments on frontier agents reveal that producing executable solvers is difficult itself, and satisfying visual and physical correctness is even harder. GPT-5.6-Sol and Claude-Opus-5 perform comparatively better than the other evaluated agents, yet achieve overall scores of only 48.7% and 46.7%, respectively. WorldSolver is an early step toward agentic solver generation, and we hope it helps drive progress toward agents that can faithfully simulate the dynamic physical world. Code is available at https://github.com/sirujiang/WorldSolver.

</details>


### [363] [Does an Agent's History Tell You When Compaction Will Hurt? A Modest, Bounded Effect on the TRACE Paired-Replay Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08722)
*Egor Pakhomov,Erik Nijkamp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探究智能体的近期行为能否预测压缩(context compaction)何时会造成伤害。基于TRACE语料库(590个AppWorld压缩边界)，发现压缩前的历史对压缩后伤害的预测能力很弱；最强触发器的AUROC达0.66，能避免21%有害边界同时保留84%压缩机会，但尚无法在与token预算规则的对比中得出结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多长时程智能体按全局规则(通常是token预算)压缩上下文，对智能体正在做什么视而不见。作者想验证：智能体近期行为能否预测压缩何时有害，从而改进压缩时机。

Method: 利用TRACE公开语料库的590个由harness触发的AppWorld压缩边界，在压缩前上下文与摘要两种条件下，从重新执行的前缀状态重放每个边界，并记录下一步动作的负担(报错或重复调用)。在此基础上检验预前缀位置对比，并评估扩展协议的触发器和冻结的可解释触发器。

Result: 压缩前历史仅微弱预测压缩后伤害；预先设定的前缀位置对比为宽泛的零结果，其"是否已写入"标签实际衡量的是轨迹阶段。最佳扩展协议触发器在留出集AUROC为0.66(复制集标签上0.64)；最佳冻结可解释触发器避免21%有害边界、保留84%压缩机会，在计数上超过随机规则但在负担质量上未超过(事后比较)。

Conclusion: 最佳触发器能否在匹配保留率下击败token预算规则，在现有数据发布上无法评估；作者据此提出了为回答该问题所需发布的语料库要求。

Abstract: Many long-horizon agents compact their context on a global rule, usually a token budget, blind to what the agent was doing. We ask whether the agent's recent behaviour predicts when a compaction will hurt. TRACE's public corpus of 590 harness-triggered AppWorld compaction boundaries replays each boundary from a re-executed prefix state under the pre-compaction context and under the summary, and records the burden of the next actions: calls that error or repeat a call already made. We find that pre-boundary history predicts post-compaction harm only weakly. An internally prespecified contrast by prefix placement is a wide null, and the naive "has-written" label behind it turns out to measure trajectory phase. The best extension-protocol trigger reaches held-out AUROC 0.66 (0.64 on the replicate's own label) against a same-boundary replicate of 0.72; the best frozen, interpretable trigger avoids 21% of harmful (positive-burden) boundaries while keeping 84% of compaction opportunities, and exceeds the random-rule expectation on count but not on burden mass (a post hoc comparison). Whether the best trigger beats a token-budget rule at matched retention cannot be evaluated on the release. We state what corpora should ship to answer it.

</details>


### [364] [VeriFine: Scaling Verification for Self-Improvement in Embodied Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08761)
*Zewei Zhou,Rachel Luo,Yulong Cao,Chaowei Xiao,Chensheng Peng,Boyi Li,Thomas Tian,Zheng Lian,Yan Wang,Jiaqi Ma,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Wenhao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 VeriFine 框架，通过策略、课程与评判器的协同演化来扩展验证能力，实现具身推理中的持续自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我改进策略不断产生新的失败模式，使固定评判器难以验证，从而限制了优化反馈和有用训练样本的发现；具身推理中的空间接地、因果推理和安全决策使该问题更严峻。

Method: VeriFine 包含两个循环：策略改进循环用评分标准评判器诊断反复出现的失败、构建自适应课程并优化策略；当进展停滞、验证成为瓶颈时，评判器改进循环选取信息量大的失败案例查询人类指导，通过人机协作校准（coactive calibration）解决分歧并逼近物理推理的客观评分标准，再用改进后的评判器指导下一阶段的数据选择与策略优化。

Result: 在驾驶和机器人导航任务上，强化学习与监督微调均展现出策略和评判器能力的持续自我改进。

Conclusion: 扩展验证能力可支持策略失败模式不断演化下的持续自我改进。

Abstract: Self-improving policies continually expose new failure patterns, changing what their judges must be able to verify. However, current fixed judges constrain both optimization feedback and the discovery of useful training examples, limiting further self-improvement. This challenge is even more acute in embodied reasoning, where reliable evaluation must account for spatial grounding, causal reasoning, and safety-aware decision-making. We introduce VeriFine, an agent harness framework that scales verification through the co-evolution of the policy, training curriculum, and judge. The Policy Improvement Loop uses a rubric judge to diagnose recurring failures, construct an adaptive curriculum, and optimize the policy. When progress plateaus and verification becomes a bottleneck, the Judge Improvement Loop selectively queries human guidance on informative failure cases and refines the judge through coactive calibration, in which humans and agents resolve disagreements and converge toward the objective rubric of physical reasoning. The revised judge then guides the next stage of data selection and policy optimization. Experiments on driving and robot navigation tasks demonstrate continuous self-improvement in both policy and judge capability across reinforcement and supervised fine-tuning. These results show how scaling verification supports continuous self-improvement as policy failure patterns evolve.

</details>


### [365] [Agent in a Bottle: Can LLM Agents Turn Their Capabilities Into Cheap, Scalable Artifacts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08775)
*Ankit Sonthalia,Haritz Puerto,Alexander Rubinstein,Martin Gubri,Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BOTTLED基准，评估LLM智能体将通用能力"装瓶"为低成本可复用方案的能力，发现零样本性能无法预测装瓶能力，但装瓶可带来显著成本节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽能解决许多窄任务，但对数百万相关实例逐一查询代价过高。因此需要评估智能体能否自主创建更便宜的解决方案，即"装瓶"能力——将通用能力转化为兼顾答案质量与摊销成本的任务专用方案。

Method: 引入BOTTLED基准：智能体接收完整无标注工作负载，须在固定时间、算力和LLM API预算下完成，自主选择方案（如训练小模型或编写可复用程序）。测试覆盖10个模型、3个任务。

Result: 强零样本性能并不能可靠转化为强装瓶能力；零样本分数相近的模型装瓶后差异显著。60次装瓶中有48次低于其模型零样本95%置信区间下界；31次低于相同token预算下两个小模型蒸馏基线中较强者的表现。但装瓶可带来大幅节省：在查询-商品相关性分类上，Opus 5以约657倍更低的报告成本保留约82%零样本macro-F1；相比专为廉价重复推理构建的"系统一"模型Jev，Opus 5以约四分之一的全负载成本恢复约94%的macro-F1。

Conclusion: BOTTLED为评估和改进智能体在大型重复工作负载中投入有限资源构建可复用解决方案的能力提供了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can solve many narrow tasks, but querying them separately for millions of related instances can be prohibitively expensive. Can LLM agents autonomously create cheaper solutions for such workloads? We call this ability "bottling": the ability to turn general capabilities into task-specific solutions that balance answer quality and amortised cost. We introduce BOTTLED, a benchmark in which agents receive an entire unlabelled workload and must complete it under fixed time, compute and LLM API budgets. Agents choose their own approach, such as training a small model or writing a reusable program. Across ten models and three tasks, we find that strong zero-shot task performance does not reliably translate into strong bottling capabilities. Models with similar zero-shot scores can differ substantially after bottling, and 48 of 60 bottling runs score below the lower bound of the 95% confidence interval of their model's zero-shot performance. Moreover, 31 of 60 runs underperform the stronger of two small-model distillation baselines with the same token budget. Nevertheless, bottling can yield substantial savings: on query-product relevance classification, Opus 5 retains about 82% of its zero-shot macro-F1 at roughly 657 times lower reported cost. Bottling is also competitive with Jev, a "system one" model built especially for cheap, repetitive inference: Opus 5 on the same task recovers about 94% of Jev's macro-F1 at a quarter of Jev's projected full-workload cost. BOTTLED provides a basis for evaluating and improving agents' ability to invest limited resources in reusable solutions for large, repetitive workloads.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [366] [EDAP: An Efficient Data-Gathering Protocol for Wireless Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06911)
*Arash Nasiri Eghbali,Nastooh Taheri Javan,Mehdi Dehghan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出高效数据收集协议EDAP，通过虚拟汇聚节点解决有向扩散协议的晚聚合与重复洪泛问题，并利用多路径实现负载均衡，网络寿命比DD提升达75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向扩散（DD）虽用数据聚合与网内处理抑制额外开销，但无法保证邻近源路径在少数跳后汇聚，且每个源都需独立洪泛探索数据包（ED），对邻近源而言存在冗余。这两点导致晚聚合与不必要的洪泛浪费，能量效率低下。

Method: 提出EDAP协议：在源附近设置虚拟汇聚（VS）节点充当汇聚角色，广播局部兴趣消息并将汇聚数据路由至目的节点；同时在VS与真实汇聚节点间建立多条（不相交或编织）路径，依据路径长度和剩余能量分配数据，实现负载均衡。

Result: 仿真结果表明，EDAP能显著节省能量并大幅延长网络寿命，相比DD最多提升约75%。

Conclusion: 通过虚拟汇聚节点与多路径负载均衡机制，EDAP有效缓解了晚聚合和重复ED洪泛问题，提升了能量效率与网络生存时间。

Abstract: Directed Diffusion (DD), uses mechanisms such as data aggregation and in-network processing to suppress the additional data overhead however there is no guarantee that paths from nearby sources join after a few hops. Also for path construction, each source floods a distinct exploratory data (ED) packet through the network which is not necessary for nearby sources. In this paper an efficient data-gathering protocol (EDAP) is proposed to address the problems of late-aggregation and distinct ED-flooding through using a virtual sink (VS) node nearby the sources which plays the role of sink node and broadcasts local interest messages and routed gathered data toward destination. Also in this protocol, multiple paths are constructed between virtual sink and the sink node which leads to load-balancing and increase in the life-time of the network nodes. Our routing protocol can produce more than one disjoint or braided path and spread the gathered data properly between constructed paths according to their length and remaining energy. Simulation results shows that in this way a significant amount of energy can be saved and the network lifetime will be increased considerably (up to 75% better than DD).

</details>


### [367] [Who Can Safely Wait? Risk-Aware 5G Scheduling for Low-Altitude UAV Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07101)
*André Melo,Yueqi Chen,Lourenço Pereira,Igor Kadota*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Risk-Aware (RA)框架，将信息新鲜度和操作上下文转化为无人机专属的操作风险，实现基于操作后果的上行调度，显著降低风险并支持更大机队规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝网络为地面用户设计，对低空飞行器信息延迟的操作后果"视而不见"；需将信息新鲜度与操作上下文转化为无人机专属风险，按操作后果而非仅网络指标进行调度。

Method: RA框架：以监管启发的风险代理、基于边际风险增长的风险导向优先级，以及带Lyapunov性能保证的Max-Weight调度器，在异质单/多时隙更新的服务成本与进一步延迟后果间取得平衡。

Result: 在圣保罗配送和Interlagos F1场景，48个操作点、最多120架无人机下，RA相比比例公平降低99分位单机风险达96%，相比轮询降低86%，并在同一风险范围内容纳1.5倍和3倍更大机队。

Conclusion: RA将信息新鲜度与操作上下文映射为操作风险，实现风险导向的调度，在真实轨迹、动态场景与视频流量下大幅降低风险并提升机队容量。

Abstract: Cellular-connected UAV operations already fly in many countries. However, cellular networks were designed for terrestrial users and remain blind to the operational consequences of delaying information from low-altitude aircraft. We present Risk-Aware (RA), a framework that translates information freshness and operational context into aircraft-specific operational risk, enabling uplink scheduling based on operational consequence rather than network metrics alone. RA balances the consequence of further delay against the service cost of heterogeneous single- and multi-slot updates. It combines a regulation-inspired risk proxy, risk-directed prioritization based on marginal risk growth, and a Max-Weight scheduler with Lyapunov-based performance guarantees. Although RA is derived from an analytically tractable model, we evaluate it under moving UAV trajectories, dynamic operational context, geographically varying population exposure, and realistic UAV video traffic. Across 48 operating points and fleets of up to 120 UAVs in São Paulo delivery operations and the Interlagos Formula 1 Grand Prix setting, RA reduces 99th-percentile per-UAV risk by up to 96% over proportional fair and 86% over round robin, while supporting fleets up to 1.5$\times$ and 3$\times$ larger, respectively, within the same risk envelope.

</details>


### [368] [Ofan: Optimal Load Balancing for AI Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07230)
*Sarah McClure,Evyatar Cohen,Jakob Krebs,Alexander Shpiner,Mark Silberstein,Scott Shenker,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Isaac Keslassy*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对AI工作负载的极端集合完成时间需求，论文指出脊叶树中目的无关的包喷洒方案存在结构性负载失衡，证明其队列量可达Θ(√m)，进而提出目的感知方案Ofan及适配交换机的pOfan，实现O(1)队列并大幅缩短完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工作负载对集合完成时间（CCT）有极高要求，现有包喷洒算法在满线速发送时难以高效负载均衡。作者追溯其结构性原因：在胖树中，一旦包选定上行路径，其到目的地的下行路径唯一，因此目的无关方案无法消除已造成的失衡。

Method: 论文首先证明了目的无关方案在消息大小为m时可能遭受Θ(√m)的排队。随后提出Ofan——一种基于交换机的目的感知负载均衡方案，可达到O(1)排队；并给出适配当前交换机管道架构的pOfan变体，还提供了P4实现。

Result: P4实现显示方案资源开销适中。基于Llama-3 405B参数模型的端到端FSDP2评估表明，与现有算法相比，Ofan将CCT膨胀缩短了16–39倍。

Conclusion: 目的感知的负载均衡对于消除胖树中目的无关喷洒造成的结构性失衡至关重要；Ofan和pOfan以低资源开销实现了近最优排队，并显著改善AI训练工作负载的集合完成时间。

Abstract: The extreme collective completion time (CCT) demands of AI workloads challenge existing packet spraying algorithms, which can have trouble efficiently load-balancing workloads that are sent at full line rates.
  We trace this to a structural cause: on a fat tree, once a packet picks its upward path, the downward path to its destination is unique, so destination-oblivious schemes cannot undo the imbalance it creates. We prove that such schemes can suffer from $Θ(\sqrt{m})$ queueing for messages of size $m$, thus eventually triggering rate reductions by the congestion control. Instead, we suggest Ofan, a switch-based destination-aware LB scheme that can reach $O(1)$ queueing. We also present its pOfan variant that fits the pipe-based architecture of current switches. Our P4 implementation shows that it consumes modest resources. An end-to-end FSDP2 evaluation with Llama-3 405B-parameter models shows that Ofan cuts CCT inflation by $16$--$39\times$ when compared to existing algorithms.

</details>


### [369] [High Speed Network Processing for Scientific Computing in the Open Cloud Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07377)
*Miriam Leeser,Michael Zink,Suranga Handagala,Carlos Ruben Dell'Aquila,Verena Martinez Outschoorn,Rafael Coelho Lopes de Sa*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出基于FPGA的SmartNIC架构，利用P4可编程数据平面在OCT测试床上实现线速率的网络可视化、优化与编排，并以SciTags为例验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据密集型科学计算工作流对高带宽数据传输和广域网实时监控需求日益增长，需要在不依赖主机CPU、不改动应用服务器的前提下实现在网处理。

Method: 基于AMD U280 FPGA构建SmartNIC，采用P4可编程数据平面，支持包解析、标记、遥测提取、流量引导及机器学习推理；集成SciTags标准，通过IPv6 Flow Label进行包标记，实现按SciTag值计包的高性能网络桥。

Result: 在OCT测试床上实验表明，处理延迟几乎不可感知，包处理达线速率，并能准确监控科学数据流。

Conclusion: 该FPGA SmartNIC方案验证了在广域网科学计算场景下进行在网处理的可行性，为网络可视化、优化与编排提供了无需主机干预的高效解决路径。

Abstract: This paper presents an FPGA-based SmartNIC architecture designed to address critical network challenges in data-intensive scientific computing. As scientific workflows increasingly demand high-bandwidth data movement and real-time monitoring across wide-area networks, the Open Cloud Testbed (OCT) provides an ideal platform for investigating in-network processing solutions at line rate.
  Our approach focuses on implementing network visibility, optimization, and orchestration capabilities through FPGA acceleration. The proposed system leverages P4-programmable data-plane functions to support packet parsing, marking, telemetry extraction, traffic steering, and machine learning inference, all without requiring host CPU intervention or modifications to application servers.
  As a concrete example, our framework integrates the SciTags standard, which enables associating network traffic with scientific context through packet marking. We implement a high-performance network bridge that counts packets by their SciTag values, as identified in the IPv6 Flow Label.
  Experimental evaluation on AMD U280 FPGAs in OCT demonstrates the feasibility of this approach, showing imperceptible processing latency and line-rate packet handling, while accurately monitoring scientific data flows.

</details>


### [370] [Understanding Conflict and Compatibility Conditions for Agents in AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07415)
*Arshia Zolghadr,Joao F. Santos,Imtiaz Nasim,Deniz Aytemiz,Nicholas J. Kaminski,Jacek Kibilda*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种控制论方法判定多个RAN控制代理能否共存共享同一参数，并以成对检测建立兼容性条件，提出冲突缓解策略实现兼容代理共享控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解耦移动网络暴露开放接口，不同目标的独立代理之间会产生冲突；现有冲突缓解方法仅因代理控制相同参数就认定冲突并强制仲裁，未评估其目标是否可被同时满足。

Method: 将文献中常见的阈值型代理建模为采样死区反馈控制器，用KPI目标与容差（定义可接受死区边界）表示其目标；从这些边界推导兼容性条件，并证明对任意数量代理和监控KPI，兼容性可通过成对检测确立；进而提出冲突缓解策略，允许兼容代理共享同一网络参数控制，仅在目标无法同时满足时才进行仲裁。

Result: 基于NIST的ns3-oran（ns-3扩展）进行仿真评估，结果表明：当代理目标兼容且共享可接受范围可达时，实现近乎零SLA漂移的参数共享控制；当目标不兼容时，性能与Priority Mitigation相当。

Conclusion: 该控制论兼容性判定与冲突缓解策略可在保证SLA的同时支持代理共享控制，避免不必要的仲裁，为解耦RAN多代理协调提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Disaggregated mobile networks expose open interfaces for independent agents to control the Radio Access Network (RAN). The interactions between agents with contrasting objectives can result in conflicts. However, existing conflict mitigation methods treat agents that control the same parameter as being in a conflict that requires arbitration of their actions, without assessing whether their objectives can be satisfied simultaneously. In this paper, we introduce a control-theoretic approach to determine when agents can coexist and share control of the same parameters. We model threshold-based agents often encountered in the literature as sampled deadband feedback controllers and represent their objectives through Key Performance Indicator (KPI) targets and tolerances that define acceptable deadband bounds. We derive compatibility conditions from these bounds and show that compatibility for any number of agents and monitored KPIs can be established through pairwise tests. We propose a conflict mitigation policy that allows compatible agents to share control of the same network parameters while restricting arbitration to cases where their objectives cannot be satisfied simultaneously. We evaluate our policy through simulations using NIST's ns3-oran extension of ns-3, and our results show that it enables agents to share control with near-zero Service Level Agreement (SLA) drift when their objectives are compatible and the shared acceptable range is attainable, while matching Priority Mitigation when their objectives are incompatible.

</details>


### [371] [From ASIC to Fleet: Lessons from Building and Operating a Hyperscaler NIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07644)
*Prankur Gupta,Alexander Duyck,Jakub Kicinski,Joseph Provine,Neal Peacock,Prabhakaran Ganesan,Rajiv Krishnamurthy,Chen Liu,Akshay Viswakumar,Timothy Vitkin,Jie Meng,Beatriz Padilla Hernandez,Michael Edwards,Andrei Kozlov,Viren Nathan,Tianyi Cui,Joy Chaoyue Xiong,Raul Hormazabal,Mohsin Bashir,Fred Feng,Nathan Walker,Lavin Khandelwal,Matt Maia,Milo Piazza,Mohanraj Thillainayagam,Mahmoud Mehr,Amithash Prasad,Lee Trager*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Meta 描述在数十万台生产主机上部署自研多主机网卡 fbnic 的运维基础设施，实现可靠性大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厂商多主机网卡由单主机架构改造而来，共享固件与缓冲区导致级联隔离故障（历时七年）。自研硬件虽通过物理隔离消除此问题，却将全部运维负担转移至超大规模厂商。

Method: 构建硬件在环 CI 流水线（测试固件、驱动、内核组合）；统一可观测性流水线（共置网卡与交换机计数器以做跨层故障归因）；驱动优先架构（固件消息类型少于十种）；亚 sled 粒度的固件升级编排器；限定爆炸半径至单主机切片的范围化修复自动化。

Result: 十个月内，相比同平台厂商网卡：非计划不可用性降低 12 倍，平均修复时间降低 37%，硬件更换减少 2.3 倍。

Conclusion: 自研网卡配合系统化运维基础设施，可在超大规模生产环境中显著提升可靠性、可维护性并降低硬件更换成本。

Abstract: We describe the operational infrastructure built to deploy and operate fbnic, a custom multi-host NIC, across hundreds of thousands of production hosts at Meta. Vendor multi-host NICs, designed by retrofitting single-host architectures, suffered from shared firmware and buffers that created cascading isolation failures over seven years. fbnic eliminates these through physical isolation, but shifting to in-house hardware shifts the entire operational burden to the hyperscaler. We present a hardware-in-the-loop CI pipeline testing firmware, driver, and kernel cross-products; a unified observability pipeline co-locating NIC and switch counters for cross-layer fault attribution; a driver-first architecture with fewer than ten firmware message types; a targeted firmware upgrade orchestrator at sub-sled granularity; and scoped repair automation confining blast radius to individual host slices. Over ten months, fbnic achieved a 12X reduction in unplanned unavailability, 37% lower mean time to repair, and 2.3X fewer hardware swaps compared to vendor NICs on the same platform.

</details>


### [372] [Semantic Split Inference for Remote Modulation Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07682)
*Samer Lahoud,Kinda Khawam*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出信道感知的语义分割推理用于远程自动调制识别，将分割深度与上报长度作为独立设计变量并端到端训练，实验表明中间分割实现最佳精度-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程自动调制识别需在感知节点复杂度、上报成本与识别精度之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出信道感知的语义分割推理：感知节点通过噪声链路发送语义上报，边缘服务器完成识别。分割深度与上报长度作为独立设计变量，并穿过信道进行端到端训练，与边缘/节点端推理及现有协作方案对比。

Result: 评估了感知节点模型大小、计算量、时延与能耗相对识别精度的表现。

Conclusion: 中间分割能在精度与成本间取得最佳权衡。

Abstract: Remote automatic modulation recognition balances sensing-node complexity, reporting cost and accuracy. To address this trade-off, we propose channel-aware semantic split inference: a sensing node sends a semantic report over a noisy link and the edge server completes recognition. In our model, split depth and report length are independent design variables, with end-to-end training through the channel. We compare the resulting design against basic split placements, which run inference at the edge server or at the sensing node, and against a state-of-the-art collaborative scheme. We assess sensing-node model size, computation, latency and energy against recognition accuracy. We show that intermediate splits give the best accuracy-cost trade-off.

</details>


### [373] [When Weak Reports Matter: Staged Anchored Fusion for Cooperative UAV Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07747)
*Xingqin Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出分阶段锚定恢复方法，在本地多径剔除后恢复协同感知证据，实现无损残差筛选，大幅降低弱报告上传量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地多径剔除会抹去协同感知所需的证据，需在剔除后仍恢复有用目标信息。

Method: 分阶段锚定恢复：先保留强确认，再用未使用的强锚点查询兼容的弱报告；证明任意节点数下无损残差筛选，并推导佐证律与双向成本律。

Result: 1024次五无人机投放中，相比严格共识增加30个匹配目标、3个误输出；检测数媲美单遍锚定确认，弱上传减少97.4%；等量线索/报告记录比全量上传少4.5%载荷；独立验证额外恢复4个目标且无误输出。

Conclusion: 该方法能在剔除多径的同时恢复证据，提升检测并显著降低通信开销，且经验证可靠。

Abstract: Local multipath rejection can erase evidence needed for cooperative sensing. We propose staged anchored recovery: preserve strong-only confirmations, then query compatible weak reports using unused strong anchors. For any number of sensing nodes, we prove lossless residual screening and derive corroboration and bidirectional cost laws. In 1,024 five-UAV drops, recovery adds 30 matched targets and three false outputs over strict consensus, matching one-pass anchored confirmation's detection counts while reducing weak uploads by 97.4%. Equal-sized cue/report records yield 4.5% less payload than uploading all eligible reports. Independent validation recovers four additional targets with no observed false outputs.

</details>


### [374] [ONDT: A Modular and Virtualized O-RAN Digital Twin for xApp/rApp Closed-Loop Experimentatio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07889)
*Miguel Catalan-Cid,Joan Josep Aleixendri,August Betzler,George Oikonomou,Lydia Ntzogani,David Reiss*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出O-RAN数字孪生（ONDT）框架，一个模块化、虚拟化、云原生的实验平台，用于在隔离可复现环境中验证O-RAN闭环控制算法与rApp/xApp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN架构通过RIC支持AI/ML驱动的xApp和rApp动态优化网络，但在真实环境中验证闭环控制算法存在高运营风险和部署成本，需要安全的实验框架。

Method: 提出ONDT框架，作为SNS SUNRISE-6G项目中的6G使能器，设计模块化、虚拟化、云原生架构，实现O-RAN合规的rApp/xApp入驻、RAN场景配置与初始化、KPM采集及闭环控制验证的端到端自动化工作流。

Result: 基于Juniper RIC平台和Keysight RICtest仿真框架的实验验证表明，ONDT能够提供隔离且可复现的环境，支持KPM监控、小区ON/OFF控制、自动化数据集生成及rApp/xApp验证。

Conclusion: ONDT为O-RAN闭环控制算法的自动化验证提供了有效、隔离且可复现的实验平台，有助于推动6G网络智能化发展。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) architectures extend programmability in next-generation cellular networks through Near-Real-Time and Non-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controllers (RICs), enabling AI/ML-driven xApps and rApps to optimize network behavior dynamically. However, validating automated closed-loop control algorithms in live environments presents significant operational risks and deployment costs. To address this challenge, this paper introduces the O-RAN Digital Twin (ONDT), a modular, virtualized, and cloud-native experimentation framework developed as a 6G enabler within the SNS SUNRISE-6G project. We present the ONDT architecture and its end-to-end experimentation workflows, showing how the framework automates O-RAN-compliant rApp/xApp onboarding, RAN scenario configuration and initialization, Key Performance Metric (KPM) collection, and closed-loop control validation. Experimental validation using Juniper RIC platforms and the Keysight RICtest emulation framework demonstrates ONDT ability to provide an isolated and reproducible environment for KPM monitoring, cell ON/OFF control, automated dataset generation, and rApp/xApp validation.

</details>


### [375] [IEEE 802.11bx - WLAN Intelligent Networking (WIN): Toward an AI-Ready Wi-Fi 9](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07900)
*Francesc Wilhelmi,Katarzyna Kosek-Szott,Szymon Szott,Boris Bellalta*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述了IEEE 802.11标准化中面向Wi-Fi 9的近期进展，围绕"AI作为协议、平台与流量"三个维度探讨AI就绪的Wi-Fi 9，并以AI流量差异化为案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wi-Fi 9被期望超越单纯通信，提供感知与计算等新服务；AI正主导802.11bx（WIN）修正案的制定，因此需要梳理推动AI就绪Wi-Fi 9的驱动因素与技术进展。

Method: 综述IEEE 802.11标准化中Wi-Fi 9的进展，沿三个互补维度分析：AI作为协议（应用于PHY/MAC操作）、AI作为平台（Wi-Fi基础设施提供AI计算）、AI作为流量（AI流需要新的流量处理策略），并讨论各维度的候选特性与开放挑战。

Result: 以AI作为流量范式为例，给出AI流量差异化的案例研究，探索对现有EDCA的潜在扩展以支持新的AI流量流。

Conclusion: 面向Wi-Fi 9，AI将从协议、平台、流量三个维度深度融入IEEE 802.11标准，AI流量差异化等方向需要新的机制支持，并存在诸多开放挑战。

Abstract: Wi-Fi 9 is expected to go beyond mere communication and provide new services such as sensing or computation. At this juncture, Artificial Intelligence (AI) is taking a leading role in the definition of the 802.11bx amendment, named WLAN Intelligent Networking (WIN). In this tutorial, we survey the recent progress made toward Wi-Fi 9 within IEEE 802.11 standardization, tracing the drivers and technological advances that motivate an AI-ready Wi-Fi 9. We then examine AI's role along three complementary dimensions, i.e., AI as a protocol (AI is applied to Wi-Fi's PHY/MAC operation), AI as a platform (Wi-Fi infrastructure is repurposed to provide AI computation), and AI as traffic (AI flows call for new traffic-handling policies), and discuss candidate features and open challenges along each. As a concrete illustration of the AI as traffic paradigm, we present a case study on AI traffic differentiation, where we explore a potential extension of the current Enhanced Distributed Channel Access (EDCA) to support new AI traffic flows.

</details>


### [376] [Split Skies: Operational Experiences Extending a Multi-Cloud Network Telescope into the Asia-Pacific](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08274)
*Nils Kempen,Benjamin Schumann,Ralph Holz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文将多云网络望远镜扩展到中国大陆腾讯云，揭示亚太地区IBR测量的盲点与障碍，其高流量区域性扫描在西方的观测点无法看到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云IBR研究依赖西方云服务商，导致单提供商望远镜存在系统性偏差，亚太地区成为测量盲点，需要多样化测量基础设施。

Method: 将多云网络望远镜部署到中国大陆腾讯云，分析互联网碎片化、地缘政治和提供商规则对全球测量的影响（严格准入、管理平面不可靠）。

Result: 部署后节点捕获到高流量的区域性扫描活动，这些扫描在西方观测点不可见。

Conclusion: 构建透明网络检查平面（Responsible Internet框架所需）受主权生态系统的瓶颈制约，需要社区协作努力，单靠独立全球观测难以实现。

Abstract: Comprehensive visibility into Internet Background Radiation (IBR) requires diverse measurement infrastructure. Recent work showed that cloud IBR depends on the provider making single-provider telescopes systematically biased. These studies relied entirely on Western providers and left the Asia-Pacific region as a blind spot. In this report we extend a multi-cloud network telescope onto Tencent Cloud inside mainland China. We detail how Internet fragmentation, geo-politics, and provider rules complicate global measurement through strict onboarding barriers and an unreliable management plane. Once deployed, our nodes captured high-volume regional scanning invisible to our Western vantage points. We conclude by connecting our findings to the Responsible Internet framework. This framework relies on independent global observation to build a transparent Network Inspection Plane. We show that building this plane is bottlenecked by sovereign ecosystems and requires a collaborative community effort.

</details>


### [377] [6G NeXt - Towards 6G Split Computing Network Applications: Use Cases and Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08352)
*Sergiy Melnyk,Qiuheng Zhou,Hans D. Schotten,Mandy Galkow-Schneider,Ingo Friese,Tobias Pfandzelter,David Bermbach,Louay Bassbouss,Alexander Zoubarev,Andy Neparidze,Arndt Kritzner,Enrico Zschau,Prasenjit Dhara,Steve Göring,William Menz,Alexander Raake,Wolfgang Rüther-Kindel,Fabian Quaeck,Nick Stuckert,Robert Vilter*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文介绍6G NeXt项目，针对全息通信和无人机防碰撞两个用例，提出基于分离计算与无线网络紧耦合的分布式计算基础设施架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G旨在融合物理、人类与数字世界。全息通信与无人机防碰撞两个用例对通信和计算基础设施提出异构需求，客户端分布广泛且需协同交互，全息通信还需强大处理能力，因此急需高速分布式骨干计算基础设施以及计算与无线网络的紧耦合。

Method: 文章首先阐述用例场景及其需求，随后提出实现这些需求的解决方案思路，并基于所开发的整体通信与计算架构讨论新颖的技术方法（如分离计算）。

Result: 本文给出了用例场景与需求分析，并提出相应的解决方案思路，围绕整体通信与计算架构探讨关键技术途径。

Conclusion: 为实现6G异构需求下的协同交互与高性能处理，需构建高速分布式骨干计算基础设施并实现计算与无线网络的紧耦合。

Abstract: The definition of the sixth generation mobile communication is in the full swing. Mobile communication aims for the convergence of physical, human and digital world. Research project 6G NeXt is considering two demanding use cases, holographic communications and drones anti-collision system, which set heterogeneous requirements on the communication as well as the computing infrastructure. In both use cases, the clients are widely spread in the network and are cooperatively interacting with each other. Especially for holographic communication, also high processing power is required. This makes a high-speed distributed backbone computing infrastructure, which realises the concept of split-computing, inevitable. Furthermore, tight integration between processing facilities and wireless network is required in order to provide adequate quality of service to the users. This paper illustrates the use case scenarios and their requirements. Afterwards, an appropriate solution approach to realise those is elaborated. Here, the novel technological approaches are discussed based on the developed overall communication and computing architecture.

</details>


### [378] [Demo: Closed-Loop Sionna-Isaac Sim Co-Simulation Framework for Wireless-Aware Robot Navigation over ROS 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08618)
*Yi Shen Lim,Seungeun Oh,Jihong Park*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个实时联合仿真框架，将 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 与 NVIDIA Sionna 通过 ROS 2 耦合，形成感知-行动-通信闭环，在 GPU 光线追踪下以约 60 Hz 更新信道覆盖图，并在校园数字孪生中验证了无线感知导航可消除通信中断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卸载控制环到网络的机器人会携带接收机移动，链路质量取决于其位置，因此仿真需要同时具备物理引擎与站点特定传播模型。现有仿真器尚无原生统一二者，已有耦合方式仅限于离线分析。

Method: 构建 Isaac Sim 与 Sionna 经 ROS 2 的实时联合仿真框架，闭合感知-行动-通信（PAC）环。Sionna 基于 Isaac Sim 所仿真的精确几何对基站到机器人的信道进行光线追踪（而非随机建模），并实时将信道状态反馈至控制环；GPU 追踪使覆盖图约 16 ms（~60 Hz）刷新。

Result: 在基于 OpenStreetMap 的 SUTD 校园孪生场景中，用两台 Nova Carter 机器人实现无线感知导航应用。闭环规划器可消除通信中断，相比最短路基线仅增加 7.4% 通行时间（基线 81.2 s 运行中有 7.9 s 处于断连）。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现物理引擎与站点特定传播模型的实时原生级联合仿真，证明实时光线追踪信道反馈可用于在线无线感知控制，且以较小时间代价换取通信可靠性。

Abstract: A robot that offloads its control loop to the network carries the receiver with it, so link quality is decided by where it goes. Simulating this requires both a physics engine and a site-specific propagation model at once; to our knowledge no simulator natively unifies both, with existing couplings of the two limited to offline analyses. We demonstrate a real-time co-simulation framework coupling NVIDIA Isaac Sim and NVIDIA Sionna over ROS 2 that closes the perception-action-communication (PAC) loop between them. Sionna ray-traces the base-station-to-robot channel over the exact geometry Isaac Sim simulates on, rather than modeling it stochastically, and feeds channel states back into the control loop in real time. Ray-traced on GPU, the coverage map is refreshed in ~16ms (~60 Hz), fast enough for real-time control. To showcase the framework's utility, we implement a wireless-aware navigation application in an OpenStreetMap(OSM)-derived SUTD campus twin with two Nova Carter robots: the closed-loop planner eliminates communication outage at only +7.4% traversal time over the shortest-path baseline (which spends 7.9 s of its 81.2 s run disconnected).

</details>


### [379] [Agentic RCA for Internet-Scale Services Using Constrained Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08622)
*Sayan Sinha,Vipul Harsh,B. Aditya Prakash,Vyas Sekar,Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: E4是一个面向互联网规模服务的智能体故障排查系统，采用"约束创造性"范式，通过受限DSL替代LLM自由生成，兼顾准确性、可解释性与低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统管理员需解决故障以维持服务可靠性，理想的排查系统应同时满足四个要求：(1)对已知和未知事件表达力强且高准确率；(2)大规模下成本高效；(3)可解释、能提供可操作洞察；(4)对运维人员负担低。然而现有系统（包括LLM辅助的智能体工作流及用于编写各类RCA算法的结构化框架）均无法同时满足这四点。

Method: 提出E4系统，体现"约束创造性"范式，结合LLM辅助自动化/探索与结构化方法的可解释性和效率。不让LLM智能体编写任意代码或生成任意响应，而是提供受限DSL，智能体通过简单的无循环数据流程序生成响应。该DSL配备高层次排查算子，使输出准确、可验证、可解释。

Result: 在合成与真实工作负载混合测试中，E4相较最先进方案准确率最高提升62%，同时提供更可解释的响应，成本最高降低12倍。

Conclusion: E4通过约束创造性范式实现准确、可解释、低成本的大规模故障排查，优于现有LLM辅助及结构化RCA方案。

Abstract: System administrators of Internet-scale services need to resolve failure incidents to maintain reliability of such services. Ideally, we want a troubleshooting system to be: (1) expressive to known and unknown incidents with high accuracy; (2) cost efficient at scale; (3) explainable to provide actionable insights operators can act on; and (4) entail low effort from the operators. Unfortunately, most existing systems, including emerging LLM-assisted agentic workflows and structured frameworks for authoring diverse RCA algorithms fall short of achieving all four requirements. We present E4, a novel agentic system for troubleshooting for Internet-scale services. E4 embodies the paradigm of constrained creativity that combines the best of LLM-assisted automation and exploration with the explainability and efficiency of a structured approach. Instead of allowing an LLM agent to write arbitrary code or generate arbitrary responses, we provide the agent a restricted DSL to generate its response via simple loop-free data flow programs. This DSL, equipped with high level operators for troubleshooting, makes E4's output accurate, verifiable and explainable. On a mix of synthetic and real-world workloads, E4 achieves up to 62% better accuracy compared to state-of-the-art solutions, while providing more explainable responses at up to 12x reduced cost.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [380] [Representation-Aware Modularity with a Distance-Calibrated Null Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07088)
*Taejoon Han,Dahee Kim,Junghoon Kim,Taeho Ha*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出RAM，一种表征感知的模块度目标，通过距离校准的零模型解决嵌入几何与经典零模型不匹配的问题，并给出分裂与多级贪心优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代节点嵌入捕捉超越原始拓扑的结构与语义邻近性，但用表征诱导的亲和度替换邻接矩阵、同时保留基于度的经典零模型，会导致观测项依赖几何、期望项与几何无关的零模型不匹配，使目标可能奖励本就可由表征邻近性预期的亲和度。

Method: 提出RAM：保持观测减期望原则，用度异质性与表征距离共同校准期望亲和度；分析其与经典模块度的关系，并开发分裂式与多级贪心优化器。

Result: 在7个真实数据集与合成可扩展性测试中，RAM优于仅拓扑、直接嵌入聚类和深度学习基线；在经典模块度下仍具竞争力，同时在大图上提升社区质量并高效扩展。消融实验确认度校正与距离校准均不可或缺。

Conclusion: RAM通过距离校准零模型有效缓解表征感知模块度的零模型不匹配问题，在社区质量与可扩展性上表现良好，且两个校准组件均为必要。

Abstract: Modularity is a standard objective for community detection, comparing observed intra-community connectivity with its null-model expectation. Modern node embeddings capture structural and semantic proximity beyond raw topology, but replacing adjacency with representation-induced affinity while retaining the classical degree-based null model makes the observed term geometry-dependent and the expected term geometry-agnostic. This creates a null-model mismatch: the objective may reward affinity already expected from representation proximity. This paper introduces RAM, a representation-aware modularity objective with a distance-calibrated null model. RAM preserves the observed-minus-expected principle by calibrating expected affinity with both degree heterogeneity and representation distance. We analyse its relation to classical modularity and develop divisive and multi-level greedy optimisers. Experiments on seven real-world datasets and synthetic scalability tests show that RAM improves over topology-only, direct embedding-clustering, and deep learning-based baselines, remains competitive under classical modularity while improving community quality on large graphs, and scales efficiently. Ablations confirm that both degree correction and distance calibration are essential.

</details>


### [381] [WPTokens: A Dataset for Studying the Regulation of Wikipedia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07108)
*Léo Joubert*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: WPTokens 数据集记录了法语维基百科全部修订历史中的内容级编辑，通过句子层和 wikitext 节点层的双层位置差分，为每段内容分配稳定标识符，追踪其增删改移，支持构建贡献者-内容随时间演化的时序多重图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究维基百科的治理机制需要知道谁在何时、何地对哪些内容进行了添加、重写、移动和删除。现有数据难以在内容粒度上完整追踪这些操作，因此需要构建一个覆盖完整修订历史、以内容片段为单位的数据集。

Method: 基于 2026 年 4 月的数据转储，对法语维基百科全文修订历史进行处理。采用两层位置差分：文本层以句子为单位，节点层对应 wikitext 的结构元素（模板、链接、引用、标题、表格、列表等）。每段内容在生命周期内保持稳定标识符，每次变动被记录为添加、更新、移动、移动并更新或删除五类事件之一。数据存储在带索引的 SQLite 数据库中；配套 R 包将记录转化为时序贡献者-内容多重图，并附加贡献者之间的回退（revert）关系层。

Result: 构建的数据集覆盖 275 万篇文章、1.536 亿次修订、3.426 亿个 token 及 5.737 亿次 token 事件。数据集以带索引 SQLite 数据库形式存储，并提供 R 包用于生成时序贡献者-内容多重图及回退关系层。

Conclusion: WPTokens 为内容粒度的维基百科治理研究提供了完整、可复用的数据基础。论文描述了分词、差分算法、数据模型与图构建方法，并讨论了该方法的局限性。

Abstract: Studying how Wikipedia is regulated requires knowing who adds, rewrites, moves and deletes which piece of content, when and where. WPTokens is a dataset that answers these questions over the full revision history of the French-language Wikipedia. It relies on a positional diff over two layers: a text layer made of sentences, and a node layer made of the structural elements of wikitext (templates, links, references, headings, tables, lists, etc.). Each piece of content keeps a stable identifier for its whole life, and every change to it is recorded as an addition, an update, a move, a move with update, or a deletion. Built from the April 2026 dump, the dataset covers 2.75 million articles, 153.6 million revisions, 342.6 million tokens and 573.7 million token events, stored in an indexed SQLite database. A companion R package turns these records into temporal contributor--token multigraphs, completed by a layer of revert ties between contributors. We describe the tokenization, the diff algorithm, the data model and the graph construction, and discuss the limitations of the approach.

</details>


### [382] [Aggregating User Preferences while Ensuring Equity, Diversity, and Inclusion using Graph Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07128)
*Adji Marieme Sita Cissé,Malek Mouhoub*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 提出AURORA，将公平性、多样性、包容性(EDI)约束直接嵌入图汇总过程，通过贪心粗化合并用户节点，在五个数据集上优于Borda/Condorcet等经典聚合规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典聚合规则(如Borda、Condorcet)缺乏机制防止结果系统性偏向多数群体、坍缩为同质项或低估少数群体，因此需要在聚合中处理EDI挑战。

Method: 将用户偏好建模为加权属性二分图，采用贪心粗化算法迭代合并用户节点，并强制三项结构性EDI准则：公平差距约束(ΔE)、列表内多样性约束(ILD)和群体包容约束。EDI保护直接嵌入图结构而非事后修正。

Result: 在五个数据集(MovieLens 100k/1M、libimseti.cz、Rate My Professors、OpenAlex)评估。AURORA多样性增益最大且最一致；MovieLens 100k在k=20时三项EDI指标同时优于Borda与Condorcet；Rate My Professors上ILD=0.808且女性项代表最高(60%)，公平代价适中；OpenAlex上公平差距最低(ΔE=0.031)，而Borda方法推荐零女性作者；在libimseti.cz(敏感属性存在于图两侧)未能降低公平差距。

Conclusion: 将EDI约束嵌入聚合结构比事后方法产生更稳健的公平-多样性权衡；但人口统计均等在双侧敏感属性场景下并不总是合适目标。

Abstract: Aggregating the preferences of diverse user groups into a collective outcome raises fundamental challenges of equity, diversity, and inclusion (EDI): classical aggregation rules such as Borda and Condorcet have no mechanism to prevent results from systematically favoring majority groups, collapsing onto homogeneous items, or under-representing minorities. We address this problem through EDI-constrained graph summarization. User preferences are modeled as a weighted attributed bipartite graph, and a greedy coarsening algorithm iteratively merges user nodes while enforcing three structural EDI criteria: an equity gap constraint ($ΔE$), an intra-list diversity constraint (ILD), and a group inclusion constraint. Rather than correcting fairness after aggregation, our method embeds EDI preservation directly into the graph structure. We evaluate across five datasets spanning four domains: MovieLens 100k and 1M, libimseti.cz, Rate My Professors, and OpenAlex (2018-2023). Our method, AURORA, achieves the largest and most consistent diversity gains over classical voting rules, and on MovieLens 100k at $k = 20$ it simultaneously improves all three EDI criteria over both Borda and Condorcet. On Rate My Professors, it combines high diversity (ILD = 0.808) with the highest female item representation (60%), at a moderate equity cost, and it achieves the lowest equity gap ($ΔE = 0.031$) on OpenAlex, where Borda-based methods recommend zero female authors. On libimseti.cz, the only dataset where the sensitive attribute is present on both sides of the bipartite graph, our method does not reduce the equity gap, a limitation we connect to prior findings that demographic parity is not always an appropriate target. These results demonstrate that embedding EDI constraints into aggregation structure yields more robust fairness-diversity trade-offs than post-hoc approaches.

</details>


### [383] [Forecasting the Growth of Social Media Information Cascades: Towards Human-in-the-Loop Misinformation Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07209)
*Ansh Gupta,Abhiram Gorle,Aayush Rajesh,Tsachy Weissman*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出以"早期30分钟传播树预测后续增长"为核心的信息误判分诊方法，结合结构/时间熵、回复模式与证据检索，构建人机协同审查系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限的人工审核团队需要在信息真实影响未知前，判断哪些新兴声称可能持续增长，从而优先分诊早期错误信息。

Method: 在FibVID上以首30分钟活动预测传播树后续增长，比较节点数、结构深度熵、时间到达熵及组合模型；在PHEME分析前15条回复的立场/交际/情感状态构成；在Check-COVID用倒数排名融合与句子重排检索证据。

Result: FibVID上组合模型将log增长R²从0.307提至0.323，log-MAE降2.4%；高活跃树R²由0.248升至0.395，ρ由0.394升至0.529，log-MAE降11.7%。PHEME中第15条回复中位28.8分钟；无LLM事实维度ROC-AUC 0.538，加入后0.562。Check-COVID中74.2%前五、94.3%前二十命中金证据，重排Recall@20达58.1%。

Conclusion: 早期增长预测、回复模式与证据检索可整合为依赖潜在病毒性的误信息分诊人审系统。

Abstract: Limited review teams must identify which emerging claims are likely to keep growing before their eventual reach is known. We center early misinformation triage on this continuation-forecasting problem: predicting subsequent recorded propagation-tree growth from the first 30 minutes of activity. On FibVID, we compare early node count with structural depth entropy, temporal arrival entropy, and their pair while keeping all propagation trees from each original claim in one partition. Across 352 test trees from 59 claim groups separate from training, the combined model raises $R^2$ for log-transformed future growth from 0.307 to 0.323 and reduces log-MAE by 2.4% (95% claim-bootstrap CI, -0.4% to 5.2%). The gain is especially pronounced among 97 high-activity trees: $R^2$ rises from 0.248 to 0.395, Spearman's $ρ$ from 0.394 to 0.529, and log-MAE falls by 11.7% (95% CI, -1.9% to 23.9%). Complementing the 30-minute growth forecast, we analyze the first 15 replies in 563 PHEME threads. In this cohort, the 15th reply arrives after a median of 28.8 minutes; 52.0% reach the fixed reply prefix within 30 minutes and 71.6% within one hour. Even without the LLM-generated factual-accuracy dimension, the remaining stance, communicative, and affective state composition retains cross-event ranking signal (ROC-AUC 0.538); including that dimension increases ROC-AUC to 0.562. In a separate Check-COVID evaluation of 229 claims, reciprocal-rank fusion retrieves a gold evidence document within the top five for 74.2% of claims and within the top 20 for 94.3%; sentence reranking reaches Recall@20 of 58.1%. We propose an integrated human-review system that brings these early forecasts, response patterns, and retrieved evidence together for misinformation triage relying on the potential virality of claims.

</details>


### [384] [Simulating Strategies for Defense Against Brand-Targeted Online Disinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07407)
*Bryan C. Boots*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文将品牌定向虚假信息建模为信任加权传染过程，通过多智能体仿真比较六种网络结构、三个放大级别和六种干预策略，发现无标度/影响者网络最易扩散，混合干预（预破除+可信节点激活+快速纠正）效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 品牌定向虚假信息不仅通过传播错误信念造成损害，还会侵蚀信任、放大声誉不确定性，并在网络化数字环境中持续存在。现有研究缺乏可复用的仿真框架来系统研究品牌虚假信息防御策略。

Method: 采用多智能体仿真，将品牌虚假信息建模为信任加权传染过程；设置六种网络结构、三个放大级别和六种干预策略进行对照实验。

Result: 无标度网络与影响者密集网络最易快速扩散；聚类网络和极化网络可部分遏制扩散，但放大效应会桥接社区从而突破遏制；混合干预（预破除、可信节点激活、快速纠正）总体表现最佳。

Conclusion: 论文提供了一个可复用的仿真框架，用于研究主动式品牌虚假信息防御策略。

Abstract: Brand-targeted disinformation can damage firms not only by spreading false beliefs, but by eroding trust, amplifying reputational uncertainty, and persisting across networked digital environments. This paper models brand-targeted disinformation as a trust-weighted contagion process using a multi-agent simulation across six network structures, three amplification levels, and six intervention strategies. Results show that scale-free and influencer-heavy networks are most vulnerable to rapid spread, while clustered and polarized networks provide partial containment until amplification bridges communities. Hybrid intervention combining prebunking, trusted-node activation, and rapid correction performs best overall. The paper contributes a reusable simulation framework for studying active brand disinformation defense strategies.

</details>


### [385] [Target-Aware Network Dismantling with Limited Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07926)
*Jiaqi Song,Fei Jing,Wenjie Huang,Xingqin Qi,Qingpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文通过将疫情干预建模为"目标感知网络拆解"问题，揭示传统"接触者追踪"策略在资源受限下并非最优，并提出深度强化学习框架RAIL实现自适应干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疫情期间资源有限，必须决定隔离或监测谁。传统公共卫生策略直觉上优先处理已知感染者及其直接接触者（contact-centric），但这一策略在现实资源约束下是否最优，此前缺乏系统论证，作者希望挑战这一直觉并寻找更优方案。

Method: 将干预决策问题形式化为"目标感知网络拆解"（target-aware network dismantling）；利用生成函数分析推导不同预算下的最优策略，发现存在依赖预算的干预转变；据此提出Risk-Aware Isolation Learning（RAIL），一个基于深度强化学习的框架，根据不断演化的残余网络自适应选择最优干预对象。

Result: 生成函数分析表明：当干预预算稀缺时，隔离结构中心个体比隔离感染者的直接邻居能保护更大人群；传统的邻居优先策略只有在资源增加时才变为最优。由于这一临界转变点随预算、网络拓扑和感染空间分布动态变化，静态策略必然失效。RAIL在多种合成与真实网络中一致地最大化人群保护并抑制疫情扩散。

Conclusion: 研究挑战现有流行病学直觉，证明接触中心策略在资源约束下根本次优，并提供了一个可推广的自适应干预框架，适用于公共卫生及其他复杂系统中的局部风险应对。

Abstract: During epidemics, limited resources dictate critical decisions about whom to isolate or monitor. Conventional public health strategies intuitively prioritize known infected individuals and their immediate contacts. Here, we reveal that this contact-centric approach is fundamentally suboptimal under realistic resource constraints. By formulating this challenge as target-aware network dismantling, we uncover a counter-intuitive, budget-dependent intervention transition. Generating-function analysis demonstrates that when intervention budgets are scarce, isolating structurally central individuals, rather than the immediate neighbours of the infected, protects a vastly larger population. The conventional neighbour-targeting strategy only becomes optimal as available resources increase. Because this critical transition point shifts dynamically with the intervention budget, network topology, and spatial distribution of infections, static strategies inevitably fail. To overcome this, we develop Risk-Aware Isolation Learning (RAIL), a deep reinforcement learning framework that adaptively selects optimal interventions based on the evolving residual network. Across diverse synthetic and real-world networks, RAIL consistently maximizes population protection and suppresses outbreak spread. Ultimately, our findings challenge prevailing epidemiological intuitions, establishing a generalizable framework for adaptive interventions against localized risks in public health and other complex systems.

</details>


### [386] [Benchmarking System One Models in Online Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07953)
*Federico Mazzoni,Andrea Failla*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文评估System One Models（SOM，接受自然语言上下文并输出类型化选项、概率或分数）能否支持在线内容审核，发现Jev在多基准上有竞争力并受益于检索先例，而Laya表现不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线审核系统需应对不断变化的平台政策、社区规则和历史决策，同时产出可审计并可转交人工复核的判决，因此需要评估SOM是否适用于该设定。

Method: 在五个审核基准上评估Jev；通过受控信息条件分离书面规则、检索到的先例以及候选答案空间限制的影响；并分析Jev与Laya的置信度用于选择性复核的效果。

Result: Jev与专用参考系统相当，在多项政策依据和有害内容设置中匹配或超越；Jev普遍受益于检索先例，在固定答案空间下提升政策选择准确率和有害内容判别；Laya不一致，检索常改变其阳性预测率或无违规率但不提升判别；Jev置信度在部分场景可支持选择性复核但校准不稳定，Laya置信度对错误排序作用较弱。

Conclusion: 结果表明SOM驱动的内容审核具有前景，但不同模型表现差异明显，检索与答案空间限制的影响值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Online moderation systems must apply changing platform policies, community rules, and prior decisions while producing decisions that can be audited and routed to human review. We evaluate whether System One Models, which accept natural-language context but return typed choices, probabilities, or scores, can support this setting. Across five moderation benchmarks, Jev is competitive with specialized reference systems, matching or exceeding them in several policy-grounded and harmful-content settings. We then use controlled information conditions to separate written rules, retrieved precedents, and restrictions on the candidate answer space. Jev generally benefits from retrieved precedents, improving exact policy selection and harmful-content discrimination when the answer space is held fixed. Laya is less consistent: retrieval often shifts its positive prediction rate or no-violation rate without improving discrimination. Jev confidence can support selective review in several settings, although it is not consistently calibrated; Laya confidence is less useful for ranking errors. These results show a promising future for SOM-powered content moderation.

</details>


### [387] [Understanding Interfirm AI Talent Flow Networks through Online Professional Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08264)
*Donghang Li,Yunhan Zheng,Alok Prakash,Shenhao Wang,Jinhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 基于58国约5.35亿条雇佣记录，构建2010-2022年企业间AI人才流动网络，发现AI人才流向日益集中于少数头部企业，但网络核心地位更易易主；AI网络中心性与企业价值正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI能力通常被视为企业内部积累的资源，但人才流动使AI能力跨越组织边界。作者希望从"人才流动网络"视角重新理解企业AI优势的来源，而非仅关注人才存量规模。

Method: 使用覆盖58个国家、约5.35亿条雇佣记录的数据库，重构2010-2022年企业间AI人才流动网络；分析网络的集中度与优先连接（preferential attachment）特征、核心位置的可竞争性；通过回归控制员工规模、资产和固定效应检验网络中心性与企业价值的关系，并用事件研究法（匹配相似事前轨迹企业）检验网络位置跃升后的价值变化。

Result: AI人才流入日益集中于少数领先企业，优先连接强于一般劳动力流动；但AI网络核心位置易主频率远高于非AI网络；网络位置包含超出员工规模的信息；AI网络中心性与更高企业价值相关，且网络位置大幅改善后企业价值相对匹配企业更高。

Conclusion: 企业AI优势不仅取决于积累了多少人才，还取决于其在AI人才流动网络中的位置；网络中心性具有经济意义。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence capabilities are often measured as resources accumulated within firms, yet they also circulate across organizational boundaries through worker mobility. Drawing on a database of approximately 535 million employment records across 58 countries, we reconstruct the inter-firm network of AI talent flows over 2010--2022. We find that AI talent inflows are becoming increasingly concentrated among a small set of leading firms, with stronger preferential attachment than in general labor mobility. Yet the network core remains contestable: central positions in AI networks change hands far more often than in non-AI networks. Network position also carries information that goes beyond workforce size. This network position is economically consequential: AI network centrality is associated with higher enterprise value, after accounting for workforce scale, assets, and fixed effects. Event-study estimates further indicate that sharp improvements in network position are followed by higher enterprise value relative to matched firms with similar pre-event trajectories. Together, these findings suggest that corporate AI advantage depends not only on how much talent firms accumulate, but also on where they sit in its circulation.

</details>


### [388] [Enhancing LLMs with Cognitive-Affective Personality Inference for Simulating Human Social-Psychological Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08328)
*Zhibo Deng,Dongyuan Li,Shuwen Ge,Ziqing Zhang,Ying Zhang,Renhe Jiang*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 论文提出SPIN，一种受认知-情感人格系统理论启发的零样本LLM推理流程，通过三次调用分离稳定人格与情境状态，提升社会心理学行为模拟的对齐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态人格提示直接将参与者画像与实验情境映射为回答，混淆了稳定人格特质与情境特定解读，难以准确模拟人类社会心理行为。

Method: SPIN通过三次零样本LLM调用实现结构化推理：编译任务无关的参与者核心人格、引出条件特定的认知-情感状态、基于该状态读出决策，从而复用稳定人格结构并将每次试验响应经由显式状态表征路由。

Result: 在两个重建的社会心理学研究族（不确定性推理与多元无知）及四个基座LLM上，SPIN相较空白、人口统计、叙事和思维链提示变体，整体对齐性能最强。消融与状态分析表明人格编译和结构化状态引出均有贡献，且引出状态在信息性与规范性条件下呈可解释的转变。

Conclusion: 结构化人格-状态推理可超越更丰富的人格描述或通用多步推理，改善基准层面的行为对齐。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate human participants in social and behavioral studies, yet static persona prompting typically maps a participant profile and an experimental scenario directly to a response, entangling stable dispositions with situation-specific interpretations. To address this limitation, we introduce \textbf{SPIN}, a cognitive-affective personality system-inspired inference pipeline for simulating human social-psychological behavior. Specifically, SPIN implements this structured inference process through three zero-shot LLM calls that compile a task-blind participant core, elicit condition-specific cognitive-affective states, and read out decisions from those states, thereby reusing stable personality structure while routing each trial-specific response through an explicit state representation. We evaluate SPIN on two reconstructed social-psychological study families spanning uncertainty reasoning and pluralistic ignorance, across four base LLMs. Compared with blank, demographic, narrative, and chain-of-thought prompt variants, SPIN consistently delivers the strongest overall alignment performance across base LLMs and study families. Ablations and state analyses further show that both personality compilation and structured state elicitation contribute to the gains, and that the elicited states shift interpretably across informational and normative conditions. These results suggest that structured personality-state inference can improve benchmark-level behavioral alignment beyond richer persona descriptions or generic multi-step reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [389] [SAFESHIELD: A Decision-Organization Framework for Deployment-Time Safety of Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07276)
*Xingru Zhou,Luis Sentis,Aarti Choudhary*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将部署时安全建模为"决策组织"问题，提出SAFESHIELD系统，通过四个决策职责（准入、路由、证据、放行）与可审计决策轨迹实现协调与审计，实验证明安全性不仅取决于单个护栏能力，更取决于决策如何被组织与协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部署框架虽提供日益强大的运行时护栏机制（输入审核、路由、检索验证、输出过滤），但缺乏对安全决策如何被显式组织、协调和审计的指导，忽略决策的组织结构会导致安全性不足。

Method: 提出以责任导向分解安全决策+显式协调为核心的决策组织框架，并实例化为面向小语言模型的SAFESHIELD系统：组织准入、路由、证据、放行四类决策职责，并将已提交决策记录为可审计的Decision Traces。评估包括机制级实验、总体阶段消融、受控协调消融和部署导向压力测试。

Result: 机制级结果表明实例化护栏具备决策过程所需能力；总体消融显示去除安全组织后端到端安全性大幅下降；协调消融中移除准入门控显著增加错误放行，切断上游证据使放行准确率从96.0%降至69.5%。

Conclusion: 部署时安全不仅依赖个体护栏的能力，还依赖其决策如何被组织与协调，提供了系统级证据支持这一观点。

Abstract: Deployment-time safety of language models is commonly implemented through runtime guardrails such as input moderation, routing, retrieval verification, and output filtering. Existing deployment frameworks provide increasingly capable mechanisms for these functions, but offer limited guidance on how the safety decisions they produce should be explicitly organized, coordinated, and audited. We formulate deployment-time safety as a decision-organization problem with two elements: responsibility-oriented decomposition of safety decisions and explicit coordination among them. We instantiate this formulation in SAFESHIELD, a deployment-time safety system for small language models that organizes four recurring decision responsibilities (admission, routing, evidence, and release) and records committed decisions in auditable Decision Traces. We evaluate SAFESHIELD through mechanism-level experiments, aggregate stage ablations, controlled coordination ablations, and a deployment-oriented stress suite. Mechanism-level results show that the instantiated safeguards provide the capabilities required by the decision process, while aggregate ablations show substantial degradation in end-to-end safety as the surrounding safety organization is removed. More importantly, dedicated coordination ablations preserve the participating safeguard mechanisms while selectively severing their dependencies: removing admission gating substantially increases false release, and withholding upstream evidence from the release decision reduces release accuracy from 96.0% to 69.5%. These results provide system-level evidence that deployment-time safety depends not only on the capability of individual guardrails, but also on how their decisions are organized and coordinated.

</details>


### [390] [Catching Developers in the Flow: Low-Latency Agentic Program Repair at Google Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07289)
*Celal Ziftci,Spencer Greene,Ray Liu,Livio Dalloro,Lorenzo Dini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FlowAgent 是部署于 Google 的 AI agent，在持续集成系统的 pre-submit 阶段实时自动修复测试失败，采用 ReAct 式生成-验证循环与预/后执行弃权过滤，在严格延迟约束下提供高质量修复建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动修复程序失败耗时且打断开发者工作。现有基于大语言模型的自动程序修复主要针对 post-submit 离线流程，缺乏满足实时低延迟需求的能力，无法在开发者切换上下文前于其工作流中提供帮助。

Method: 提出 FlowAgent，集成于 Google 内部开发者工具 Critique 和 Cider。采用 ReAct 风格 generate-and-validate 循环，并引入严格的预执行与后执行弃权过滤器（abstention filters），在严格延迟约束下保证建议质量。

Result: 对 195 个真实测试失败的人工评估显示修复建议准确率为 67.18%。Google 全公司部署后，FlowAgent 在 295,508 次变更上给出建议，开发者预览 65,069 次、采纳应用 28,554 次。访谈反馈表明该 agent 有助于提供正确修复。

Conclusion: 自主修复 agent 融入工业软件工程工作流受到良好接受，但仍存在有趣挑战与机会。

Abstract: Manual repair of program failures is time-consuming and disruptive for software developers, particularly during the pre-submit phase where test failures occur within continuous integration systems. While Automated Program Repair has seen significant advancement through Large Language Models, existing state-of-the-art techniques primarily focus on post-submit workflows, operating offline without the low-latency requirements necessary to assist developers in real-time within their flow before they switch context.
  In this paper, we introduce FlowAgent, an AI agent deployed at Google to automatically repair test failures in the pre-submit outer-loop workflow inside continuous integration systems. Integrated into Google's internal developer tools, Critique and Cider,FlowAgent utilizes a ReAct-style generate-and-validate loop, as well as rigorous pre-execution and post-execution abstention filters to ensure high-quality suggestions under strict latency constraints.
  Based on our case studies, FlowAgent is highly effective. First, a manual evaluation conducted on 195 real-world test failures demonstrated 67.18% accuracy in suggesting correct fixes. Following its Google-wide deployment, FlowAgent suggested fixes on 295,508changes, of which developers previewed 65,069 and applied 28,554. Developer feedback from interviews indicate that the agent is useful in suggesting correct fixes, integration of autonomous repair agents into industrial software engineering workflows is received well, while interesting challenges and opportunities still remain.

</details>


### [391] [A Validated Dataset and Benchmark for Coherent Multi-Diagram SysML Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07356)
*Ardalan Aryashad,Yan Jin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出SEMAADB，一个包含3000个工程语境、15000张SysML图的系统工程项目数据集与基准，并评估大模型生成连贯多图的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统工程师需共同使用多种图（需求、块定义、活动、状态机、时序）描述系统结构，需保持一致。大语言模型可生成图文本或代码，但其生成连贯图集的能力尚不清楚，且缺乏大规模评测数据集与基准。

Method: 构建SEMAADB数据集：包含3000个工程语境、15000张图，每个语境含五类相互关联的SysML视图。对图集进行一致性与渲染有效性检查，其中100个语境经人工验证构成基准测试集。在“图修复”和“跨图更新”两个任务上评估三个语言模型。

Result: 最强模型修复64.3%的语义错误；跨图更新中最佳传播F1为80.7%。语法修复几近解决，但语义修复和跨图一致性仍具挑战。

Conclusion: SEMAADB为衡量连贯多图SysML生成提供了大规模图资源与基准，显示语义与一致性能力仍是模型短板。

Abstract: Systems engineers use several diagrams to describe the structure and behavior of systems. Engineers create these diagrams together to make sure that they use the same elements and remain consistent with one another. Large language models can generate diagrams as text or code, which makes it possible to create system diagrams automatically. However, their ability to generate coherent sets of diagrams is not well understood, and existing datasets and benchmarks do not directly measure this ability at scale. We introduce SEMAADB (Systems Engineering Modeling Assistant with AI Dataset and Benchmark), a dataset of 3,000 engineering contexts and 15,000 diagrams. Each context contains five connected SysML views: Requirement, Block Definition, Activity, State Machine, and Sequence. Here, a view is a diagram that presents one aspect of a system. We checked the diagram sets for consistency and valid rendering. A set of 100 contexts is also human-verified and forms the benchmark test set. We evaluate three language models on two tasks. In diagram repair, the strongest model repairs 64.3% of semantic errors . In cross-diagram update the best propagation F1 is 80.7% when a model applies one change across related diagrams. The results show that syntax repair is nearly solved, but semantic repair and consistency across diagrams are still challenging tasks for models. SEMAADB therefore provides both a large diagram resource and a set of benchmarks for measuring coherent multi-diagram SysML generation.

</details>


### [392] [CogAdapt: Cognition-informed Sparse Adaptation of Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07446)
*Yueke Zhang,Zihan Fang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CogAdapt利用人类EEG与眼动数据学习先验，指导代码模型进行任务相关的稀疏块级适配，仅更新被选中的transformer块，在降低86%以上可训练参数的同时提升代码生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成模型性能提升依赖昂贵的全参数微调；人类对齐学习虽引入认知信号，但通常仍适配大部分模型，训练成本几乎不变。作者假设人类认知信号不仅指示模型该学什么，还能指示"在哪里"适配最有用。
