Method: 提出CogAdapt框架：先从人类EEG和注意力数据学习可迁移的程序级与token级先验，再结合冻结模型对每个编码任务的响应，决定分配多少适配量以及哪些transformer块需要更新；微调时只更新被选中的块，推理时无需新的脑电数据。

Result: 在Qwen和GLM上发现人类阅读行为与MoE计算存在一致对应关系；CogAdapt在LiveCodeBench和BigCodeBench上取得最佳pass@1，较常规微调在LiveCodeBench上分别提升10.86和6.29个百分点，同时将可梯度更新的适配参数减少86.21-87.21%。

Conclusion: 人类理解信号可为代码模型适配提供有效指导，使适配更具选择性和有效性，实现更低成本与更强性能的统一。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become increasingly capable of generating code. However, achieving stronger code-generation performance still often relies on costly model adaptation, i.e., fine-tuning pretrained model parameters. Prior studies have shown correspondence between human code processing and neural models' attention or internal computation. Human-aligned learning approaches use cognitive signals to guide training, but typically adapt a large portion of the model, leaving training costs largely unchanged. Human cognitive signals may indicate not only what the model must learn from, but also where adaptation is most useful. We investigate whether human responses during code reading correspond to code-model behavior and can guide selective adaptation without sacrificing performance.
  We present CogAdapt, a cognition-informed framework for task-dependent sparse adaptation of code models. CogAdapt first learns transferable program-level and token-level priors from human Electroencephalography (EEG) and attention data, then combines these priors with the frozen model's response to each coding task to determine how much adaptation to allocate and which transformer blocks should receive updates. During fine-tuning, only the selected blocks are updated, while no new human recordings are required for inference. Across Qwen and GLM, we find consistent correspondence between human reading behavior and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) computation. CogAdapt achieves the best pass@1 across both LiveCodeBench and BigCodeBench, including gains of 10.86 and 6.29 percentage points over matched regular fine-tuning on LiveCodeBench, while reducing gradient-eligible adaptation parameters by 86.21-87.21%. These results suggest that human comprehension signals can provide useful guidance for making code-model adaptation both more selective and more effective.

</details>


### [393] [The EPIC Framework for Spec-Driven Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07534)
*Bruno Claudino,Ruizhe Xing,Yuanhao Zuo,Tyler Menezes,Anita Sarma,Kostadin Damevski,Zixuan Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究优秀SDD规范应包含什么，基于ISO/IEC/IEEE 29148评分114个开源SDD仓库并提炼出EPIC框架（40项实践、10个质量维度），帮助开发者在编码代理行动前发现并填补规范空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 措辞选择影响代理行为（如"must"与"should"之别），代理会用自身假设填补指令空白。SDD要求开发者先写规范、计划、任务再让代理写代码，但现有模板无法帮助判断规范是否足够充分或清晰。

Method: 用ISO/IEC/IEEE 29148对114个开源SDD仓库的产出物进行评分，从高分仓库中提炼实践，形成EPIC框架（包含10个质量维度的40项实践）；并通过15位SDD实践者的反馈及仓库质量分层的提交数据分析进行验证。

Result: 多数实践者（N=15）认可每一项实践；规范质量前三分之一的仓库，bug修复提交占比11.8%，后三分之一为20.4%；前者的贡献者中位数为后者的4倍。

Conclusion: EPIC可帮助开发者在代理之前发现并填补规范中的空白，提升规范质量并带来更低的缺陷修复负担与更多协作贡献。

Abstract: One practitioner we interviewed said their team writes "must" instead of "should" when instructing a coding agent, because the agent may treat "should" as optional. Small wording choices matter because agents often fill gaps in their instructions with their own assumptions. Spec-driven development (SDD) asks developers to write a specification, plan, and tasks before the agent writes code. SDD frameworks provide templates for these artifacts, but the templates do not help developers judge whether they have written enough or clearly enough. We studied what good SDD specifications contain. We scored the artifacts of 114 open-source SDD repositories against ISO/IEC/IEEE 29148 and derived practices from the highest-scoring ones. The resulting framework, EPIC, has 40 practices in 10 quality dimensions that guide developers in making expectations and decisions explicit in specifications, plans, and tasks for coding agents. The majority of SDD practitioners (N=15) endorsed every practice. Repositories in the top third of specification quality spent 11.8% of their commits on bug fixes, compared with 20.4% in the bottom third, and had a median of 4x as many contributors. Developers can use EPIC to find and fill the gaps in a specification before the agent does.

</details>


### [394] [CheckerBench: Can Long-Horizon Agents Synthesize Static-Analysis Checkers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07557)
*Hang He,Li Wang,Hao Chen,Yuchen Shao,Yuling Shi,Lisheng Wang,Peiyang Liu,Goose Lin,Zaiyuan Wang,Haiying Sun,Ting Su,Chengcheng Wan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 CheckerBench（300 个源自 CVE 的可执行静态分析检查器合成任务）与 CheckerLab 评估框架，测试 coding agent 端到端开发可用 checker 的能力，发现最佳配置 Pass@1 仅 45.33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 coding-agent benchmark 多聚焦补丁生成或漏洞检测，很少评估 agent 能否从零到一在仓库中开发出可工作的静态分析 checker，且缺乏可执行、可复现的评测基准。

Method: 构建 CheckerBench：包含 300 个任务，来自 297 个 CVE、167 个仓库、85 个 CWE、5 种语言生态；每个任务提供漏洞版与修复版修订、固定分析环境和 checker 脚手架。配套 CheckerLab 统一评估框架，独立重建提交的 checker，并度量漏洞-修复诊断对比度、补丁定位、误报及工具使用情况。

Result: 在 21 种模型-框架配置、每种 3 次独立重复下，平均 Pass@1 为 32.30%，最佳配置为 45.33%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，可靠、可复用的 checker 开发对当前 coding agent 仍是显著挑战，CheckerBench 为该方向提供了可执行评测基础。

Abstract: Static-analysis checker synthesis requires agents to interpret a defect specification, inspect a repository, implement analyzer-specific logic, and refine the checker through repeated compilation and analysis feedback. Existing coding-agent benchmarks focus on tasks such as patch generation or vulnerability detection and rarely assess whether an agent can develop a working checker in a repository from start to finish. We introduce CheckerBench, an executable benchmark of 300 tasks derived from 297 CVEs across 167 repositories, 85 CWEs, and five language ecosystems. Each task includes vulnerable and fixed revisions, a pinned analysis environment, and a checker scaffold. We further introduce CheckerLab, a common evaluation framework that independently rebuilds submitted checkers and measures vulnerable-fixed diagnostic contrast, patch localization, false positives, and tool use. Across 21 model-harness configurations and three independent repeats per configuration, mean Pass@1 is 32.30%, while the best reaches 45.33%. These results show that reliable, reusable checker development remains challenging for current coding agents.

</details>


### [395] [When Old Facts Return: Re-Reads, Reverts, and the Limits of Temporal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07715)
*Neeraj Yadav*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究记忆系统中"旧语句重现"带来的歧义：同一个观测序列可能来自重新读取旧源，也可能来自真正的回退，但两者需要的当前答案相反。作者在130个原子转换上评估，发现逐字重读会大幅降低准确率，并提出了一个防护机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆系统可能仅因相同的旧语句再次出现，就"退休"一个过时值随后又"恢复"它。重新读取旧源与真正的回退会产生相同的观测值序列，却要求相反的当前答案——现有系统难以区分这种歧义。

Method: 在130个由提取器选出的软件修复原子转换上研究该歧义。对比普通转换条件下的基于身份的时间记忆表现。引入"逐字重读旧语句"的构造条件，并测试一个"拒绝重新激活已退休值"的防护机制。另有两个支持性研究：向答案模型暴露已退休历史、以及为变更行为提供当前源。另有707个软件修复的探索性提取研究提供范围背景。

Result: 普通条件下，基于身份的时间记忆达到98.5%模型判定准确率，在字面陈旧值代理下零观测错误。加入逐字重读后准确率降至10.8%，陈旧值率升至88.5%。防护机制在该构造的重读条件下将准确率恢复到97.7%，陈旧值率降至0.8%。但该防护无法在缺乏额外变更来源信息的情况下识别合法的回退。

Conclusion: 设计启示是：应区分"对某值的观测"与"该值已改变的证据"。局限性包括：选定的输入、仅聚合答案记录、相关族判定者及事后防护评估，使结论仅限于所保留的实验。

Abstract: A memory system can retire an obsolete value and later restore it merely because the same old statement appears again. A re-read of an old source and a genuine revert can produce the same observed sequence of values while requiring opposite current answers. We study this ambiguity on 130 extractor-selected atomic transitions derived from software fixes. In the ordinary transition condition, identity-based temporal memory reaches 98.5% model-judged accuracy with zero observed errors under a literal stale-value proxy. Appending a verbatim re-read of the old statement reduces accuracy to 10.8% and raises the stale-value rate to 88.5%. A guard that refuses to reactivate a previously retired value restores accuracy to 97.7% and reduces that rate to 0.8% in this constructed re-read condition. The guard cannot also recognize a legitimate revert without additional change provenance. Two supporting studies examine exposing retired history to the answer model and supplying current source for changed behavior. An exploratory extraction study over 707 software fixes provides scope context, not a universal coverage estimate. The design implication is to distinguish an observation of a value from evidence that the value changed. Selected inputs, aggregate-only answer records, related-family judges and a post-failure guard evaluation limit the conclusions to the retained experiments.

</details>


### [396] [Acquiring and Verifying Repository Norms for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07757)
*Kaifeng He,Xiaojun Zhang,Zhenxi Chen,Christoph Treude,Mingwei Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 RepoNorm，独立于编码任务获取仓库显式与隐式规范，验证内容与适用性后向编码代理交付规范包，显著提升合规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理产出的改动虽能通过功能测试，却常违反仓库的贡献规范。仓库规范分散各处且含条件与例外，现有检索与文档方法只提供通用上下文，代理仍难判断哪些规范适用。

Method: RepoNorm 独立于编码任务获取仓库的显式与隐式规范；利用仓库证据校验规范内容与适用性，必要时查询 Git 历史，并将规范包交付给现有编码代理。评估使用 3 个编码模型和 RepoNormBench 的 121 个任务，与无额外生成指引的 Raw 基线及 CodeWiki 文档对比。

Result: 相对 Raw：整体规范合规率（NCR）提升 7.42-10.77%，贡献 NCR 提升 31.64-45.44%，提示遗漏 NCR 提升 11.34-17.69%；三个模型在上述指标均优于 CodeWiki。功能成功率比 Raw 高 5.79-9.09 个百分点，但配对比较未达显著阈值。默认配置的采样精确率为 86%。

Conclusion: 独立于任务的规范获取与适用性校验能有效提升编码代理对仓库贡献规范的遵守程度，且优于文档式方案；功能成功率提升趋势积极但需更多验证。

Abstract: Changes produced by coding agents can pass functional tests while leaving repository contribution requirements unmet. Following repository-specific norms requires identifying guidance dispersed across repository sources and interpreting its conditions and exceptions. Retrieval and documentation approaches supply general context, but agents must still determine which norms apply. We introduce RepoNorm to acquire explicit and implicit repository norms independently of coding tasks. It checks norm content and applicability using repository evidence, consults Git history when needed, and delivers norm packages to existing coding agents. Our evaluation uses three coding models and 121 tasks from RepoNormBench. Against the baseline with no additional generated guidance (Raw), relative improvements are 7.42-10.77% for Overall Norm Compliance Rate (NCR), 31.64-45.44% for Contribution NCR, and 11.34-17.69% for Prompt-omitted NCR. All three coding models also obtain higher values on these NCR measures with RepoNorm than with CodeWiki documentation. Functional success rates show observed gains of 5.79-9.09 percentage points over Raw; the paired comparisons do not reach the significance threshold. Sampled precision is 86% with RepoNorm's default configuration.

</details>


### [397] [ES-Trace: Auditing Ethical-Sourcing Disclosure of Code Generation Models Beyond Model Cards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07762)
*Zhuolin Xu,Haibo Wang,Shin Hwei Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ES-Trace框架，通过模型文档溯源图（MDTG）追踪模型卡之外的证据，审计26个模型的伦理来源披露；发现仅审计模型卡显著低估披露程度，且社会与劳工方面披露仍严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成模型广泛用于软件开发，但其伦理来源问题（知识产权、隐私、公平性、劳工实践、环境影响）令人担忧。已有工作定义了伦理来源标准，但尚不清楚现有模型实际披露了多少证据、这些证据位于何处。

Method: 提出ES-Trace审计框架，基于模型文档溯源图（MDTG）表示模型、版本与文档工件之间的关系，从而追踪模型卡之外的披露证据。对10个发布者的26个模型、77份文档、20个伦理来源方面（ES-CodeGen）进行审计。

Result: 仅审计模型卡平均得分1.77/5，解析声明的引用后升至2.82/5，增量主要来自发布者在结构化元数据中声明的文档。主要发现：(1)社会与劳工相关方面即使在完整文档范围内也记录不足；(2)解析引用可显著提高观察到的披露；(3)仅模型卡审计会误判发布级别的披露变化；(4)文档不匹配会将证据关联到错误的模型或版本。

Conclusion: 呼吁采用引用感知的伦理来源披露审计、显式的模型—版本绑定及文档工件间的一致性，并加强对目前披露不足的社会与劳工方面的记录。

Abstract: Code generation models have been increasingly used in software development, but their development raises ethical-sourcing concerns involving intellectual property, privacy, fairness, labour practices, and environmental impact. Although prior work has defined ethical-sourcing criteria for code generation, it remains unclear how much evidence existing models disclose and where that evidence can be found. We introduce ES-Trace, a framework for ethical-sourcing disclosure audits that traces disclosed evidence beyond model cards using the Model Documentation Traceability Graph (MDTG), which represents relationships among models, versions, and documentation artifacts. We apply ES-Trace to 26 models from 10 publishers across 77 documents and 20 ES-CodeGen aspects. Model-card-only auditing yields a mean score of 1.77/5, while resolving the declared references increases it to 2.82/5, with most of the increase arising from documents that the publisher declares in structured metadata. The key findings of our study include: (1) social and labour-related aspects remain poorly documented, even when expanding the audit to the full documentation scope, (2) resolving documentation references substantially increases observed disclosure, raising the mean score from 1.77/5 to 2.82/5, (3) model-card-only audits can mischaracterize release-level disclosure changes, and (4) documentation mismatches can associate evidence with the wrong model or version, highlighting the need for explicit model--version binding and consistency across documentation artifacts. Our study calls for reference-aware ethical-sourcing disclosure audits, explicit model--version binding and consistency across documentation artifacts, and stronger documentation of currently underreported social and labour-related aspects.

</details>


### [398] [Harness Engineering for Software Engineering via Modular Executable Dev-Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07832)
*Haibo Jin,Xinjie Li,Peng Kuang,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 Dev-Primitives 抽象与 HERMES 框架，将仓库组件与 LLM 结合为可执行智能体接口，缓解长时程软件工程任务中的上下文爆炸与语义漂移，性能平均提升12.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具备终端访问能力的大模型智能体在长时程软件工程工作流中表现脆弱：需要反复重建分散于源码、配置、测试、依赖和运行时行为中的程序状态，导致交互历史过长、上下文爆炸和语义漂移；大型仓库更增加了任务相关组件识别的难度。

Method: 提出 Dev-Primitives：将仓库制品与常驻 LLM 配对，使制品获得基于自身实现与依赖的智能体原生接口，支持自然语言推理、组件间通信和局部自修改。基于此构建 HERMES 框架，通过依赖感知的动态激活机制在仓库规模上实例化这些原语，并用缺陷诊断机制将执行证据映射回需修改的组件。

Result: 在四个软件工程基准测试上，HERMES 平均优于匹配基线框架12.4%。配合强激活与诊断模型时，即使使用 Qwen3-8B Dev-Primitives，也在四个基准上均保持在同质 GPT-5.6 Sol 配置的4.5%以内，同时将 Terminal-Bench 4.0 的推理成本降低26.2%。

Conclusion: 框架（harness）设计对软件工程智能体至关重要，模块化可执行的 Dev-Primitives 与 HERMES 能有效提升长时程任务的表现并降低成本。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) equipped with terminal access have demonstrated strong capabilities in automating software engineering tasks. However, existing agents remain brittle on long-horizon workflows, where they must repeatedly reconstruct program state scattered across source files, configurations, tests, dependencies, and runtime behavior, leading to increasingly long interaction histories, context explosion, and semantic drift. Large repositories further complicate the identification of task-relevant components. To address these challenges, we introduce \textbf{Dev-Primitives} (\emph{Development Primitives}), a modular and executable abstraction that transforms repository components from passive software artifacts into active participants in software engineering. Each Dev-Primitive pairs a repository artifact with a resident LLM, which gives the artifact an agent-native interface grounded in its own implementation and dependencies, enabling natural-language reasoning, inter-component communication, and localized self-modification. Building on Dev-Primitives, we propose \textbf{HERMES}, a Harness Engineering framework for software engineeRing via Modular Executable Dev-PrimitiveS, which instantiates these primitives at repository scale through a dependency-aware dynamic activation mechanism and a bug diagnosis mechanism that maps execution evidence back to the components that must be revised. Extensive experiments on four software engineering benchmarks demonstrate that HERMES outperforms matched baseline harnesses by 12.4\% on average. Moreover, when paired with strong activation and diagnosis models, HERMES, even with Qwen3-8B Dev-Primitives, remains within 4.5\% of the homogeneous GPT-5.6 Sol configuration across all four benchmarks, while reducing inference cost by 26.2\% on Terminal-Bench 4.0, highlighting the importance of harness design in software engineering agents.

</details>


### [399] [Beyond the Leaderboard: Multi-Dimensional Evaluation of Dense and Mixture-of-Experts Models for Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08173)
*Anvi Kalpesh Shah,Umamaheswara Sharma B*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出加权质量指数(QI)评估语言模型自动程序修复,综合正确性、可维护性、安全性和生成效率;发现排名随权重变化,MoE模型以更少激活参数达到与7B/14B密集模型相当的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有APR评估通常仅以补丁是否通过测试套件为标准,忽视了可维护性、安全性和计算成本等质量维度的差异,且单指标评估会掩盖模型间的权衡关系。

Method: 受ISO/IEC 25010软件质量模型启发,构建可配置权重的加权质量指数(QI),融合功能正确性、可维护性、安全性和生成效率。评估三个密集Qwen2.5-Coder模型(3B、7B、14B)及DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite MoE模型(16B参数,2.4B激活)于40个QuixBugs和90个Defects4J缺陷上,均在相同硬件本地运行以控制基础设施影响,并用McNemar精确检验比较正确性。

Result: 模型排名随加权方案变化,说明单指标评估会掩盖权衡;MoE模型与7B、14B密集模型的正确性差异几乎不具统计显著性,但激活参数少3-6倍;而三个密集模型规模增大时正确性显著提升。

Conclusion: 对于稀疏代码模型(MoE),激活参数量比总参数量更能反映其能力,可作为更有信息量的评估视角;多维度加权评估有助于揭示APR模型间的真实权衡。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) with language models is usually evaluated by whether a generated patch passes the test suite, which can hide differences in maintainability, security, and computational cost. We propose a Weighted Quality Index (QI), inspired by the ISO/IEC 25010 software quality model, that combines functional correctness, maintainability, security, and generation efficiency under configurable weighting schemes. We evaluate three dense Qwen2.5-Coder models (3B, 7B, 14B) and the 16B-parameter DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model (2.4B active parameters) on 40 QuixBugs and 90 Defects4J bugs, all run locally on identical hardware to control for infrastructure effects. Model rankings change with the weighting scheme, showing that single-metric evaluation can hide trade-offs. The MoE model shows almost no statistically significant difference in correctness from the 7B and 14B dense models (McNemar's exact test) while using 3-6 times fewer active parameters, whereas correctness increases significantly across the three dense scales. These results suggest that active parameter count can be a more informative lens than total parameter count for sparse code models.

</details>


### [400] [Newer and Bigger, but Safer? A Longitudinal Study of the Functionality-Security Gap in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08240)
*Thiago Santos de Moura,Fynn Matuschek,Flavio Toffalini,Yannic Noller*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对7个模型家族、32个LLM进行纵向研究，考察代码生成的“功能-安全差距”。发现新模型绝对安全性提升但无家族能消除差距；开源与闭源并非决定因素，紧凑模型通常更不安全，且存在CWE级别的持续弱点和跨语言风险差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码常能通过功能测试却存在安全漏洞，即“功能-安全差距”。已有研究仅覆盖三个模型家族，并得出“模型更聪明但并未更安全”、唯一开源家族停滞的结论。该结论是否适用于其他（尤其紧凑型）模型家族尚不清楚，因此需扩大范围的纵向研究。

Method: 对7个模型家族（其中5个开源）共32个LLM进行纵向研究，每家族涵盖三个连续版本，包括旗舰版与紧凑版。使用CWEval基准，包含5种编程语言、119个任务、31种CWE，跨家族、模型规模和语言比较差距轨迹。

Result: 新模型绝对安全性提升，但没有任何家族消除差距。与先前工作不同，开源并非区分因素：所有考察的开源家族都显著缩小差距，而Gemini 3.1 Pro的差距与Llama一样宽。紧凑模型通常不如旗舰版安全，但有例外（如Gemini 3.7 Flash）。CWE层面确认了日志注入（CWE-117）和HTTP响应拆分（CWE-113）等持续弱点，最新闭源模型在内存和整数类弱点上出现回归，同一CWE在不同语言中风险差异很大。

Conclusion: 开发者不应假设升级会提升安全性，也不应假设闭源模型更安全；每次更换模型后都应重新运行安全检查，并在模型上下文中提供安全API。研究结果对LLM厂商、研究者和开发者均有启示。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used to generate code. Although their functional plausibility keeps improving, the generated code often contains security vulnerabilities. The functionality-security gap captures code that passes functional tests but fails security tests. A recent longitudinal study of three model families concluded that LLMs become smarter but not safer, with the only considered open-weight family stagnating. Whether this holds for other (open-weight) families and particularly for compact models remains open. We present a longitudinal study of the gap across 32 LLMs from seven model families (five open-weight), covering three successive releases per family in flagship and compact variants. Using CWEval with 119 tasks in five programming languages and 31 CWEs, we compare trajectories across families, model sizes, and languages. Newer models do become safer in absolute terms, although no family closes the gap. Unlike prior work, we find that openness does not separate the families: every considered open-weight family narrows the gap significantly, while Gemini 3.1 Pro keeps a gap as wide as the one reported for Llama. Compact models usually produce less secure code than their flagship counterparts, with notable exceptions (e.g., Gemini 3.7 Flash). At the CWE level, we confirm persistent weaknesses such as log injection (CWE-117) and HTTP response splitting (CWE-113) and regressions in the newest proprietary models on memory and integer weaknesses, and show that the same CWE carries very different risk across languages. From these results, we derive implications for LLM vendors, researchers, and developers. In particular, developers should assume neither that upgrades improve security nor that proprietary models are more secure; they should rerun security checks after each model change and provide secure APIs in the model's context.

</details>


### [401] [Learning from Failures: A Failure-Driven Prompt Refinement for LLM-Based Vulnerability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08405)
*Mandana Ghadamian,David Mohaisen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出"失败驱动提示精炼"(FDPR)方法，通过分析LLM在漏洞分析中的反复失败模式来系统化改进提示设计，并在DVJA和Juliet测试集上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要用聚合性能指标比较提示策略，难以解释模型为何失败，也缺乏系统化改进提示的方法。

Method: 在DVJA上识别反复出现的失败模式(假阳性、假阴性、无依据推理、CWE误分类)，将其转化为针对性的提示改进，再在Juliet测试集上评估并进行跨模型验证。

Result: 失败驱动的精炼提升了LLM漏洞分析的可靠性，并提炼出可复用的提示设计原则；跨模型验证显示出泛化性。

Conclusion: 反复出现的模型失败为提示工程提供了有原则的基础，可系统化构建更可靠的LLM漏洞分析系统。

Abstract: Large Language Models have emerged as promising tools for software vulnerability analysis, but their effectiveness depends heavily on prompt design. Existing research primarily compares prompting strategies using aggregate performance metrics, providing limited insight into why models fail or how prompts can be improved systematically. We propose Failure-Driven Prompt Refinement (FDPR), a methodology that analyzes recurring model failures to guide evidence-based prompt refinement. Using the Damn Vulnerable Java Application (DVJA), we identify recurring failure modes, including false positives, false negatives, unsupported reasoning, and CWE misclassification, and translate them into targeted prompt refinements. We then evaluate the resulting prompt on the Juliet Test Suite and perform cross-model validation to assess generalizability. The results show that failure-driven refinement improves the reliability of LLM-based vulnerability analysis while yielding reusable prompt design principles. More broadly, this work demonstrates that recurring model failures provide a principled foundation for prompt engineering, enabling the systematic development of more reliable LLM-based vulnerability analysis systems.

</details>


### [402] [RAPO-Sol: Retrieval-Augmented Preference Optimization for Repository-Level Solidity Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08429)
*Rongcun Wang,Shi Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出RAPO-Sol两阶段框架用于仓库级Solidity代码生成：训练时用检索增强微调(RAFT)注入合约模式，再用基于语义锚点扰动(SAP)的DPO优化偏好，推理时无需检索。在三个模型上均验证RAFT+DPO组合效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solidity合约管理资产、权限与不可逆状态，代码生成既实用又安全关键。仓库级生成需跨状态变量、修饰符、事件、继承、外部调用和访问控制逻辑保持一致，难度大，现有方法难以胜任。

Method: 两阶段训练框架RAPO-Sol：①RAFT为训练输入附加相似Solidity示例，学习合约级复用模式，推理时去除检索；②DPO让模型偏好参考合约优于"接近但有语义缺陷"的替代品，被拒样本通过SAP构造——扰动验证语句、可见性修饰符、数据位置关键字、上下文变量、支付操作和底层调用。

Result: 在SolidityBench上，用CodeLlama-7B-Instruct、DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct测试：RAFT稳定优于SFT；SAP-DPO进一步提升BLEU和SolidityScore；完整RAFT+DPO在三模型上均最优。

Conclusion: 训练时检索与Solidity感知偏好优化收益互补，且不增加推理开销，证明该两阶段方案对仓库级Solidity生成有效。

Abstract: Smart contracts written in Solidity manage assets, permissions, and irreversible state changes, making code generation both useful and security-critical. Repository-level Solidity generation is challenging because models must synthesize complete contracts or libraries while preserving consistency across state variables, modifiers, events, inheritance, external calls, and access-control logic. We present RAPO-Sol, a two-stage training framework for repository-level Solidity code generation. First, Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning (RAFT) augments each training input with similar Solidity examples, helping the model learn recurring contract-level patterns while remaining retrieval-free at inference time. Second, Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) trains the model to prefer reference contracts over close but semantically flawed alternatives. We construct rejected samples using Solidity Semantic-Anchor Perturbation (SAP), which perturbs validation statements, visibility modifiers, data-location keywords, context variables, payment operations, and low-level calls. Experiments on SolidityBench with CodeLlama-7B-Instruct, DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct, and Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct show that RAFT consistently improves over supervised fine-tuning, while SAP-based DPO provides further gains in BLEU and SolidityScore. The full RAFT+DPO pipeline achieves the best performance across all three models, demonstrating complementary benefits from retrieval during training and Solidity-aware preference optimization without adding retrieval cost at inference.

</details>


### [403] [A Case Study in Assuring AI-Written Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08651)
*Lindsey Ferris,Sierra Bonilla*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过编码代理构建医疗平台案例，提出人类主导的"元代理"系统，指出测试、监控和审查代理均可能失效，人类控制需将意图、证据、权限与决策绑定到同一目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程代理让非专业人士也能构建系统，但产出的代码量远超人工审查能力，因此仅靠穷尽式代码审查无法可靠实现人类控制。论文通过对一个无正式软件工程训练的操作者治理的生产级医疗平台案例研究，探索何种机制才能真正维持人类控制。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，跟踪一个通过编码代理构建的生产级医疗平台。该平台工作流逐渐演化为人类主导的元代理系统：一个代理写代码，其他代理监督和审查，项目规则承载经验教训并传承。操作者在此基础上实践并观察各类监督机制的运行与失效情况。

Result: 操作者发现测试、监控和审查代理都不可靠：部分监控测量的是代理指标而非真实结果，部分审计静默失败，缺失的检查从报告结果中消失，一次自动修复还引发了运营中断。

Conclusion: 在这一案例中，人类控制的关键在于将预期结果、用于判断的证据、代理权限以及最终人类决策四者绑定到同一底层目标上；单一审查或监督机制不足以作为人类控制的可靠基础。

Abstract: Software-engineering agents can enable people without formal software training to build systems they could not otherwise implement and simultaneously can produce more code than even experts can meaningfully inspect. In both cases, exhaustive code review is not reliable as the sole basis for human control. We report a case study of a production healthcare platform built through coding agents and governed by an operator without formal software-engineering training. Over time, its workflow grew into a human-led meta-agent system where one agent wrote code, other agents supervised and reviewed it, and project rules carried lessons forward. The operator found that tests, monitors and reviewing agents used to supervise the system were fallible. Some monitors measured proxies rather than outcomes, some audits failed silently, missing checks disappeared from reported results and one automated repair caused operational disruption. In this case, human control depended on keeping the intended outcome, the evidence used to judge it, the agents' permissions and the final human decision were all tied to the same underlying objective.

</details>
